===Original===
For **P. Schuyler Miller**

 who expressed a wish

 for some Apache colonists,

 and **Charles F. Kelley**

 who has a liking

 for “time agent” tales.

===Chinese===
献给**P. 舒伊勒·米勒**（P. Schuyler Miller），

他曾表达过一个心愿，

想看到一些阿帕奇殖民者的故事；

也献给**查尔斯·F. 凯利**（Charles F. Kelley），

他素来喜好

「时间代理人」题材的传奇。

## I / I

===Original===
No windows broke any of the four plain walls of the office; there was no focus of outer-world sunlight on the desk there. Yet the five disks set out on its surface appeared to glow﻿—perhaps the heat of the mischief they could cause﻿ ﻿… had caused﻿ ﻿… blazed in them.

But fanciful imaginings did not cushion or veil cold, hard fact. Dr. Gordon Ashe, one of the four men peering unhappily at the display, shook his head slightly as if to free his mind of such cobwebs.

His neighbor to the right, Colonel Kelgarries, leaned forward to ask harshly: “No chance of a mistake?”

===Chinese===
办公室四面素墙无一开窗，桌面上也没有外界阳光聚落的光斑。然而陈列在桌面上的那五盘磁盘却似有微光流转——也许是它们可能闯下的、……已经闯下的祸患，那股热力正在盘中灼灼燃烧。

但再奇诡的想象，也无法缓冲或遮蔽冷硬的事实。戈登·阿什医生（Dr. Gordon Ashe）正满怀不快地凝视着这排磁盘，他微微摇头，像是要把脑子里这类蛛网般的杂念甩脱。

他右侧的邻座凯尔加里斯上校（Colonel Kelgarries）俯身向前，厉声问道：「没有弄错的可能吗？」

===Original===
“You saw the detector.” The thin gray string of a man behind the desk answered with chill precision. “No, no possible mistake. These five have definitely been snooped.”

“And two choices among them,” Ashe murmured. That was the important point now.

“I thought these were under maximum security,” Kelgarries challenged the gray man.

===Chinese===
「侦测仪你都亲眼见了。」桌后那个细瘦得像一股灰绳的男人答道，字字冰冷精确。「没有，绝无弄错的可能。这五盘千真万确已被窃录（snoop）过。」

「而且其中有两盘是值得下手的对象。」阿什喃喃道。这才是眼下的要害。

「我还以为这些磁盘都在最高规格的安保之下。」凯尔加里斯向那灰衣人发出诘问。

===Original===
Florian Waldour’s remote expression did not change. “Every possible precaution was in force. There was a sleeper﻿—a hidden agent﻿—planted﻿—”

“Who?” Kelgarries demanded.

Ashe glanced around at his three companions﻿—Kelgarries, colonel in command of one sector of Project Star, Florian Waldour, the security head on the station, Dr. James Ruthven﻿ ﻿…

“Camdon!” he said, hardly able to believe this answer to which logic had led him.

===Chinese===
弗洛里安·瓦尔多尔（Florian Waldour）那副拒人千里的神情纹丝不动。「一切可能的防范措施都在执行之中。是有个『沉睡者』（sleeper）——一名潜伏间谍——被安插了进来——」

「是谁？」凯尔加里斯追问。

阿什环视身旁三位同僚——凯尔加里斯，「星辰计划」（Project Star）一个分区的上校指挥官；弗洛里安·瓦尔多尔，星站安全主管；还有詹姆斯·鲁思文医生（Dr. James Ruthven）……

「坎姆登（Camdon）！」他说，几乎不敢相信逻辑竟把自己引向了这个答案。

===Original===
Waldour nodded.

For the first time since he had known and worked with Kelgarries Ashe saw him display open astonishment.

“Camdon? But he was sent us by﻿—” The colonel’s eyes narrowed. “He must have been sent﻿ ﻿… There were too many cross checks to fake that!”

“Oh, he was sent, all right.” For the first time there was a note of emotion in Waldour’s voice. “He was a sleeper, a very deep sleeper. They must have planted him a full twenty-five or thirty years ago. He’s been just what he claimed to be as long as that.”

===Chinese===
瓦尔多尔点了点头。

自认识凯尔加里斯并与之共事以来，阿什头一回见他露出毫不掩饰的惊愕。

「坎姆登？可他是被派到我们这儿来的——」上校眯起了眼睛。「他必然是真派来的……当时过了那么多道交叉审查，作不了假！」

「哦，他确实是派来的，没错。」瓦尔多尔的声音里头一次透出一丝情绪。「他是个『沉睡者』，埋得极深的沉睡者。对方准是早在整整二十五乃至三十年前就把他安插了下来。这么些年里，他一直就是他自称的那个人。」

===Original===
“Well, he certainly was worth their time and trouble, wasn’t he?” James Ruthven’s voice was a growling rumble. He sucked in thick lips, continuing to stare at the disks. “How long ago were these snooped?”

Ashe’s thoughts turned swiftly from the enormity of the betrayal to that important point. The time element﻿—that was the primary concern now that the damage was done, and they knew it.

“That’s one thing we don’t know.” Waldour’s reply came slowly as if he hated the admission.

“We’ll be safer, then, if we presume the very earliest period.” Ruthven’s statement was as ruthless in its implications as the shock they had had when Waldour announced the disaster.

===Chinese===
「哼，他们花的时间心思可真没白费，不是吗？」詹姆斯·鲁思文的声音低沉粗粝，如同闷雷。他抿了抿厚厚的嘴唇，眼睛仍死死盯着那几盘磁盘。「这些是什么时候被窃录的？」

阿什的思绪飞快地从这场叛卖的深重可怕转到这个要害上。时间因素——损害既然已经铸成，这便是头等大事，他们心里都清楚。

「这恰恰是我们不知道的。」瓦尔多尔回答得很慢，仿佛极不情愿承认这一点。

「那么，往最早处估，我们才稳妥。」鲁思文这句话的言下之意冷酷无情，不亚于瓦尔多尔宣布这场灾祸时他们所受的震动。

===Original===
“Eighteen months ago?” Ashe protested.

But Ruthven was nodding. “Camdon was in on this from the very first. We’ve had the tapes in and out for study all that time, and the new detector against snooping was not put in service until two weeks ago. This case came up on the first checking round, didn’t it?” he asked Waldour.

“First check,” the security man agreed. “Camdon left the base six days ago. But he has been in and out on his liaison duties from the first.”

“He had to go through those search points every time,” Kelgarries protested. “Thought nothing could get through those.” The colonel brightened. “Maybe he got his snooper films and then couldn’t take them off base. Have his quarters been turned out?”

===Chinese===
「十八个月前？」阿什抗声道。

可鲁思文点了点头。「坎姆登从一开始就参与了此事。这十八个月里，那些记忆磁带（tapes）一直被调进调出供研究之用，而新装的防窃录侦测仪两周前才投入使用。第一轮例行检查就查出了这桩案子，对吧？」他问瓦尔多尔。

「第一轮。」安全主管应道。「坎姆登六天前离开了基地。不过他打从一开始就常因联络差事进出往来。」

「他每次都得经过那些检查站。」凯尔加里斯抗辩道，「我还以为什么东西都休想从那里过。」上校精神一振。「也许他拍下了窃录胶片，却一直没能带出基地。他的住处彻底翻查过了吗？」

===Original===
Waldour’s lips lifted in a grimace of exasperation. “Please, Colonel,” he said wearily, “this is not a kindergarten exercise. In confirmation of his success, listen﻿…” He touched a button on his desk and out of the air came the emotionless chant of a newscaster.

“Fears for the safety of Lassiter Camdon, space expediter for the Western Conference Space Council, have been confirmed by the discovery of burned wreckage in the mountains. Mr. Camdon was returning from a mission to the Star Laboratory when his plane lost contact with Ragnor Field. Reports of a storm in that vicinity immediately raised concern﻿—” Waldour snapped off the voice.

“True﻿—or a cover for his escape?” Kelgarries wondered aloud.

“Could be either. They may have deliberately written him off when they had all they wanted,” Waldour acknowledged. “But to get back to our troubles﻿—Dr. Ruthven is right to assume the worst. I believe we can only insure the recovery of our project by thinking that these tapes were snooped anywhere from eighteen months ago to last week. And we must work accordingly!”

===Chinese===
瓦尔多尔的嘴角撇出一个恼怒的怪相。「拜托，上校，」他倦然说道，「这可不是幼儿园的练习。要证实他已得手，请听……」他按下桌上的一个按钮，播音员毫无起伏的播报声凭空响起。

「外界对西方会议航天理事会（Western Conference Space Council）航天事务督办拉西特·坎姆登（Lassiter Camdon）安危的忧虑，已因山中发现烧毁的飞机残骸得到证实。坎姆登先生自星辰实验室（Star Laboratory）执行任务返航途中，座机与拉格诺机场（Ragnor Field）失去联络。据报该区域有风暴，随即引发关注——」瓦尔多尔啪的一声掐断了声音。

「是真坠机——还是掩护他脱身的幌子？」凯尔加里斯出声嘀咕。

「两者皆有可能。等榨干了想要的一切，他们或许就蓄意把他一笔勾销了。」瓦尔多尔承认道。「不过还是回到我们的麻烦上来——鲁思文医生按最坏处着想是对的。我认为，唯有假定这些记忆磁带从十八个月前到上周之间的任何时点都可能已被窃录，才能确保项目得以挽回。而我们必须照此行事！」

===Original===
There was silence in the room as they all considered that. Ashe slipped down in his chair, his thoughts enmeshed in memories. First there had been Operation Retrograde, when specially trained “time agents” had shuttled back and forth in history, striving to locate and track down the mysterious source of alien knowledge which the eastern Communistic nations had suddenly begun to use.

Ashe himself and a younger partner, Ross Murdock, had been part of the final action which had solved the mystery, having traced that source of knowledge not to an earlier and forgotten Terran civilization but to wrecked spaceships from an eon-old galactic empire﻿—an empire which had flourished when glacial ice covered most of Europe and northern America and Terrans were cave-dwelling primitives. Murdock, trapped by the Reds in one of those wrecked ships, had inadvertently summoned its original owners, who had descended to trace﻿—through the Russian time stations﻿—the looters of their wrecks, destroying the whole Red time-travel system.

But the aliens had not chanced on the parallel western system. And a year later that had been put into Project Folsom One. Again Ashe, Murdock, and a newcomer, the Apache Travis Fox, had gone back into time to the Arizona of the Folsom hunters, discovering what they wanted﻿—two ships, one wrecked, the other intact. And when the full efforts of the project had been centered on bringing the intact ship back into the present, chance had triggered controls set by the dead alien commander. A party of four, Ashe, Murdock, Fox, and a technician, had then made an involuntary voyage into space, touching three worlds on which the galactic civilization of the far past was now marked only by ruins.

===Chinese===
满室默然，人人都在掂量这句话的分量。阿什瘫坐在椅子里，思绪缠陷于回忆之中。最初是「逆行行动」（Operation Retrograde）：受过专门训练的「时间代理人」往返穿梭于历史之中，力图查明并追踪东方共产主义各国突然开始使用的那个外星知识的神秘来源。

阿什本人和更年轻的搭档罗斯·默多克（Ross Murdock）都参与过揭开谜底的最后行动：他们查明，那个知识源头并非某个早被遗忘的地球文明，而是来自一个亘古银河帝国的失事飞船——那个帝国鼎盛之时，冰川还覆盖着欧洲和北美洲的大部分地区，地球人（Terran）仍是穴居的原始人。默多克被赤色分子困在其中一艘失事飞船里，无意间唤来了飞船原来的主人。飞船人降临追踪——循着俄国人的时间站（time station）一路清剿劫掠其失事船的盗掘者——把赤色分子的整套时间旅行体系捣了个粉碎。

但外星人并未撞见平行的西方体系。一年之后，那套体系投入了「福尔松一号计划」（Project Folsom One）。阿什、默多克，再加上新加入的阿帕奇族青年特拉维斯·福克斯（Travis Fox），再度回溯时间，去到福尔松猎手时代的亚利桑那，找到了他们想要的东西——两艘飞船，一艘失事，一艘完好。而当项目的全部力量都集中起来，要将那艘完好的飞船运回现代时，偶然的机缘触发了死去的外星人指挥官预设的操纵装置。于是，阿什、默多克、福克斯外加一名技师的四人小队身不由己地进行了一次太空航行，到访了三个世界——远古的银河文明在那里如今只余废墟可寻。

===Original===
Voyage tape fed into the controls of the ship had taken the men, and, when rewound, had﻿—by a miracle﻿—returned them to Terra with a cargo of similar tapes found in a building on a world which might have been the central capital for a government comprised not of countries or of worlds but of solar systems. Tapes﻿—each one the key to another planet.

And that ancient galactic knowledge was treasure such as the Terrans had never dreamed of possessing, though there were the attendant fears that such discoveries could be weapons in enemy hands. There had been an enforced sharing with other nations of tapes chosen at random at a great drawing. And each nation secretly remained convinced that, in spite of the untold riches it might hold as a result of chance, its rivals had done better. Right at this moment, Ashe did not in the least doubt, there were agents of his own party intent on accomplishing at the Red project just what Camdon had done there. However, that did not help in solving their present dilemma concerning Operation Cochise, one part of their project, but perhaps the most important now.

Some of the tapes were duds, either too damaged to be useful, or set for worlds hostile to Terrans lacking the equipment the earlier star-traveling race had had at its command. Of the five tapes they now knew had been snooped, three would be useless to the enemy.

===Chinese===
是输进飞船操纵系统的航行磁带把他们带走了；磁带倒转之后，又奇迹般地把他们送回了地球，还捎回了一整批同样的磁带——那是在某个世界的一栋建筑里找到的，那个世界或许曾是一个政府的中央首都，而那个政府的组成单位既非国家、亦非行星，而是一个个星系。一盘盘磁带——每一盘都是通往另一颗行星的钥匙。

那份远古银河知识是地球人做梦也未曾想过能拥有的宝库，但随之而来的也有恐惧：这样的发现一旦落入敌人之手便会成为武器。后来各大国公开大抽签，随机选出的磁带被迫与他国分享。而每个国家都暗自认定，纵然自己凭运气捞到了说不尽的宝藏，对手们必定拿得更多。此时此刻——阿什毫不怀疑——己方阵营的特工正一门心思，要在赤色分子的项目里干一票堪与坎姆登在此地的作为比肩的勾当。然而这丝慰藉无助于解决他们眼下的两难——关于「科奇西行动」（Operation Cochise），项目的一个组成部分，或许如今是最重要的部分。

有些磁带是废品，要么破损太甚无从利用，要么所对应的世界对地球人充满敌意——地球人可没有先前那个星际旅行种族所掌握的装备。如今已知遭窃录的那五盘磁带里，有三盘对敌人毫无用处。

===Original===
But one of the remaining two﻿ ﻿… Ashe frowned. One was the goal toward which they had been working feverishly for a full twelve months. To plant a colony across the gulf of space﻿—a successful colony﻿—later to be used as a steppingstone to other worlds﻿ ﻿…

“So we have to move faster.” Ruthven’s comment reached Ashe through his stream of memories.

“I thought you required at least three more months to conclude personnel training,” Waldour observed.

Ruthven lifted a fat hand, running the nail of a broad thumb back and forth across his lower lip in a habitual gesture Ashe had learned to mistrust. As the latter stiffened, bracing for a battle of wills, he saw Kelgarries come alert too. At least the colonel more often than not was ready to counter Ruthven’s demands.

===Chinese===
可余下的两盘里，有一盘……阿什皱起了眉头。那正是他们整整十二个月来狂热奋斗的目标：在太空深渊的另一端植下一片殖民地——一片成功的殖民地——日后用作通往其他世界的跳板……

「所以我们必须加快动作。」鲁思文的话穿过阿什的回忆之流传进耳中。

「我还以为你至少还需要三个月才能完成人员训练。」瓦尔多尔说道。

鲁思文抬起一只肥胖的手，粗大的拇指指甲沿着下唇来回刮蹭——这个习惯性动作，阿什早已学会不去轻信。阿什绷紧了身子，准备迎接一场意志的较量，同时瞥见凯尔加里斯也警觉起来。至少，这位上校多半是随时准备回击鲁思文的要求的。

===Original===
“We test and we test,” said the fat man. “Always we test. We move like turtles when it would be better to race like greyhounds. There is such a thing as overcaution, as I have said from the first. One would think”﻿—his accusing glance included Ashe and Kelgarries﻿—“that there had never been any improvising in this project, that all had always been done by the book. I say that this is the time we must take the big gamble, or else we may find we have been outbid for space entirely. Let those others discover even one alien installation they can master and﻿—” his thumb shifted from his lip, grinding down on the desk top as if it were crushing some venturesome but entirely unimportant insect﻿—“and we are finished before we really begin.”

There were a number of men in the project who would agree with that, Ashe knew. And a greater number in the country and conference at large. The public was used to reckless gambles which paid off, and there had been enough of those in the past to give an impressive argument for that point of view. But Ashe, himself, could not agree to a speedup. He had been out among the stars, shaved disaster too closely because the proper training had not been given.

“I shall report that I advise a takeoff within a week,” Ruthven was continuing. “To the council I shall say that﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我们测试啊测试，」胖子说道，「没完没了地测试。明明该像灵缇一样飞奔，我们却像乌龟一样爬行。世上是有『过分谨慎』这回事的，我从一开始就这么说。人家还以为」——他那谴责的目光把阿什和凯尔加里斯都扫了进去——「这个项目里从来没有过临机应变，一切都照本宣科。我说，现在正是我们必须豪赌一场的时候，否则就会发现太空已经整个儿被别人抢占了。让对手哪怕只掌握一座他们能驾驭的外星人设施，那么——」他的拇指从唇边移开，狠狠碾在桌面上，仿佛碾死一只胆大妄为却无足轻重的小虫——「那么我们还没真正起步就完蛋了。」

项目里有不少人会赞同这话，阿什心里清楚。在全国、乃至整个西方会议之内，附和的人更多。公众早已习惯了那些孤注一掷却大获全胜的豪赌，过去这类例子够多，足以为那种观点提供一套言之凿凿的论据。但阿什本人无法赞同提速。他到过群星之间，就因为受训不足，与灾祸贴身擦过、险死还生。

「我将上报建议一周之内起飞。」鲁思文继续说道，「对理事会我要说——」

===Original===
“And I do not agree!” Ashe cut in. He glanced at Kelgarries for the quick backing he expected, but instead there was a lengthening moment of silence. Then the colonel spread out his hands and said sullenly:

“I don’t agree either, but I don’t have the final say-so. Ashe, what would be needed to speed up any takeoff?”

It was Ruthven who replied. “We can use the Redax, as I have said from the start.”

Ashe straightened, his mouth tight, his eyes hard and angry.

===Chinese===
「我可不同意！」阿什插了进来。他指望凯尔加里斯立刻声援，瞥了过去，等来的却是一段越拖越长的沉默。末了，上校摊开双手，闷声道：

「我也不同意，可拍板的不是我。阿什，要把起飞提前，需要做些什么？」

接话的是鲁思文。「可以用『雷达克斯』（Redax），我从头就这么主张。」

阿什挺直了身子，嘴唇紧抿，目光又冷又怒。

===Original===
“And I’ll protest that﻿ ﻿… to the council! Man, we’re dealing with human beings﻿—selected volunteers, men who trust us﻿—not with laboratory animals!”

Ruthven’s thick lips pouted into what was close to a smile of derision. “Always the sentimentalists, you experts in the past! Tell me, Dr. Ashe, were you always so thoughtful of your men when you sent agents back into time? And certainly a voyage into space is less a risk than time travel. These volunteers know what they have signed for. They will be ready﻿—”

“Then you propose telling them about the use of Redax﻿—what it does to a man’s mind?” countered Ashe.

“Certainly. They will receive all necessary instructions.”

===Chinese===
「这一点我要抗议……要告到理事会去！老兄，我们面对的是人——是挑选出来的志愿者，是信任我们的人——不是实验室里的动物！」

鲁思文厚厚的嘴唇一撇，几乎挂出一丝讥诮的笑。「你们这些钻研过去的专家，永远是多愁善感！告诉我，阿什医生，你把特工们送回过去的时候，可曾回回都这么体贴手下人？何况一次太空航行的风险肯定比时间旅行小。这些志愿者明白自己签下的是什么。他们会准备好的——」

「那么你打算把启用雷达克斯的事告诉他们——告诉他们那东西对人的心智意味着什么？」阿什反唇相讥。

「当然。一切必要的说明他们都会得到。」

===Original===
Ashe was not satisfied and he would have spoken again, but Kelgarries interrupted:

“If it comes to that, none of us here has any right to make final decisions. Waldour has already sent in his report about the snoop. We’ll have to await orders from the council.”

Ruthven levered himself out of his chair, his solid bulk stretching his uniform coveralls. “That is correct, Colonel. In the meantime I would suggest we all check to see what can be done to speed up each one’s portion of labor.” Without another word, he tramped to the door.

Waldour eyed the other two with mounting impatience. It was plain he had work to do and wanted them to leave. But Ashe was reluctant. He had a feeling that matters were slipping out of his control, that he was about to face a crisis which was somehow worse than just a major security leak. Was the enemy always on the other side of the world? Or could he wear the same uniform, even share the same goals?

===Chinese===
阿什仍不罢休，还要再言，凯尔加里斯却打断了他：

「说到底，我们哪个都没资格做最终决定。瓦尔多尔已经把窃录案的报告呈上去了。只能等理事会的命令。」

鲁思文撑着扶手从椅子里起身，敦实的块头把制服连体工作服撑得鼓鼓的。「不错，上校。同时我建议各位都去查一查，自己那一摊子活计还能如何提速。」说罢再无一言，迈着重步走向门口。

瓦尔多尔望着另外两人，不耐烦渐浓。显然他有公务在身，只想他们快走。阿什却恋恋难去。他有一种感觉：事态正滑出他的掌控，他将要面对一场危机，一场不知怎的比重大泄密更糟的危机。敌人当真永远站在世界的另一端吗？抑或，他也可以穿同一身制服，甚至怀抱同一套目标？

===Original===
In the outer corridor he still hesitated, and Kelgarries, a step or so in advance, looked back over his shoulder impatiently.

“There’s no use fighting﻿—our hands are tied.” His words were slurred, almost as if he wanted to disown them.

“Then you’ll agree to use the Redax?” For the second time within the hour Ashe felt as if he had taken a step only to have firm earth turn into slippery, shifting sand underfoot.

“It isn’t a matter of my agreeing. It may be a matter of getting through or not getting through﻿—now. If they’ve had eighteen months, or even twelve﻿ ﻿… !” The colonel’s fingers balled into a fist. “And *they* won’t be delayed by any humanitarian reasoning﻿—”

===Chinese===
到了外面的走廊他仍迟疑不决，先行一两步的凯尔加里斯不耐烦地回头望他。

「争也没有用——我们被捆住了手脚。」他的话音含混，几乎像要同这些话撇清干系。

「那你会同意启用雷达克斯了？」一个钟头之内这是第二回，阿什恍觉自己才迈出一步，脚下的坚土便化作了溜滑游移的流沙。

「问题不在我同不同意。如今这关头，问题在于干得成还是干不成。要是他们已经捞足了十八个月，哪怕只是十二个月……！」上校的手指攥成了拳头。「而*他们*可不会被任何人道主义的道理拖住脚——」

===Original===
“Then you believe Ruthven will win the council’s approval?”

“When you are dealing with frightened men, you’re talking to ears closed to anything but what they want to hear. After all, we can’t prove that the Redax will be harmful.”

“But we’ve only used it under rigidly controlled conditions. To speed up the process would mean a total disregard of those controls. Snapping a party of men and women back into their racial past and holding them there for too long a period﻿ ﻿…” Ashe shook his head.

“You have been in Operation Retrograde from the start, and we’ve been remarkably successful﻿—”

===Chinese===
「那么你认为鲁思文会赢得理事会的批准？」

「跟一帮吓破了胆的人打交道，你说的再有理，他们除了想听的什么也听不进。再说了，我们也证明不了雷达克斯一定有害。」

「可我们以往只在严格受控的条件下使用过它。提速就意味着把那些控制措施统统抛到脑后。把一批男女猛地拽回他们种族的过去，而且羁留在那儿太久……」阿什摇了摇头。

「你从开创之初就在逆行行动里，而我们的成绩斐然——」

===Original===
“Operating in a different way, educating picked men to return to certain points in history where their particular temperaments and characteristics fitted the roles they were selected to play, yes. And even then we had our percentage of failures. But to try this﻿—returning people not physically into time, but *mentally and emotionally* into prototypes of their ancestors﻿—that’s something else again. The Apaches have volunteered, and they’ve been passed by the psychologists and the testers. But they’re Americans of today, not tribal nomads of two or three hundred years ago. If you break down some barriers, you might just end up breaking them all.”

Kelgarries was scowling. “You mean﻿—they might revert utterly, have no contact with the present at all?”

“That’s just what I do mean. Education and training, yes, but full awakening of racial memories, no. The two branches of conditioning should go slowly and hand in hand, otherwise﻿—real trouble!”

“Only we no longer have the time to go slow. I’m certain Ruthven will be able to push this through﻿—with Waldour’s report to back him.”

===Chinese===
「那是用另一套路子：把精选出来的人教育培养之后再送回历史的某些节点，让他们各自的性情禀赋恰好匹配被选去扮演的角色——是这么回事吧。可即便如此，我们也有一定比例的失败。而眼下要尝试的这个——不是把人的肉体送回时间，而是把他们*在心智与情感上*送回祖先的原型——那完全是另一码事。阿帕奇人是自愿报的名，也通过了心理学家和测验官的审核。可他们是当今的美国人，不是两三百年前的部落游牧民。有些屏障一旦砸开一道口子，到头来很可能就全数崩塌。」

凯尔加里斯眉头紧锁。「你是说——他们可能彻底退化，与当下完全失去联系？」

「我正是这个意思。教育与训练，可以有；种族记忆的全面唤醒，不行。这两条调教的路子必须缓缓推进、齐头并进，否则——真会出大乱子！」

「只可惜我们已经没有慢慢来的时间了。我敢肯定，鲁思文能推动这事通过——还有瓦尔多尔的报告给他撑腰。」

===Original===
“Then we’ll have to warn Fox and the rest. They must be given a choice in the matter.”

“Ruthven said that would be done.” The colonel did not sound convinced of that.

Ashe snorted. “If I hear him telling them, I’ll believe it!”

“I wonder whether we can﻿ ﻿…”

Ashe half turned and frowned at the colonel. “What do you mean?”

===Chinese===
「那我们就得警告福克斯和其他人。这件事必须让他们有选择的余地。」

「鲁思文说会这么办的。」上校的口气可听不出半点信服。

阿什嗤了一声。「得亲耳听见他跟他们说了，我才信！」

「我在想我们究竟能不能……」

阿什半转过身，朝上校皱起眉头。「你什么意思？」

===Original===
“You said yourself that we had our failures in time travel. We expected those, accepted them, even when they hurt. When we asked for volunteers for this project we had to make them understand that there was a heavy element of risk involved. Three teams of recruits﻿—the Eskimos from Point Barren, the Apaches, and the Islanders﻿—all picked because their people had a high survival rating in the past, to be colonists on widely different types of planets. Well, the Eskimos and the Islanders aren’t matched to any of the worlds on those snooped tapes, but Topaz is waiting for the Apaches. And we may have to move them in there in a hurry. It’s a rotten gamble any way you see it!”

“I’ll appeal directly to the council.”

Kelgarries shrugged. “All right. You have my backing.”

“But you believe such an effort hopeless?”

===Chinese===
「你自己说过，时间旅行里我们有过失败。那些失败我们早有预料，也认了，哪怕代价惨痛。为这个项目招募志愿者时，我们就必须让他们明白其中含着极重的风险。三支新兵队伍——巴伦角（Point Barren）来的爱斯基摩人、阿帕奇人，还有岛民（Islanders）——全都是因为各自的族裔在过去拥有极高的生存记录才被挑中，去当形形色色行星上的殖民者。这么说吧，爱斯基摩人和岛民跟那几盘被窃录磁带所对应的任何一个世界都对不上号，可托帕兹（Topaz，意即『黄玉』）正等着阿帕奇人。说不定我们得火速把他们送进去。不管你怎么看，这都是一场糟糕透顶的豪赌！」

「我要直接向理事会申诉。」

凯尔加里斯耸了耸肩。「行。我支持你。」

「可你觉得这番努力全无指望？」

===Original===
“You know the red-tape merchants. You’ll have to move fast if you want to beat Ruthven. He’s probably on a straight line now to Stanton, Reese, and Margate. This is what he has been waiting for!”

“There are the news syndicates; public opinion would back us﻿—”

“You don’t mean that, of course.” Kelgarries was suddenly coldly remote.

Ashe flushed under the heavy brown which overlay his regular features. To threaten a silence break was near blasphemy here. He ran both hands down the fabric covering his thighs as if to rub away some soil on his palms.

===Chinese===
「那些官老爷的做派你还不清楚。想抢在鲁思文前头，你动作得快。他这会儿八成正直接去找斯坦顿（Stanton）、里斯（Reese）和马盖特（Margate）。这正是他等着的时机！」

「还有各大报业辛迪加呢；舆论会站在我们这边——」

「你当然不是当真的。」凯尔加里斯陡然冷了下来，拒人千里。

阿什端正的五官上覆着一层深棕色的粗粝肤色，此刻那棕色底下泛起了红。在这地方，扬言捅破缄默差不多算是渎神之举。他双手顺着裤料往下抹了抹大腿，仿佛要搓掉掌心的什么污渍。

===Original===
“No,” he replied heavily, his voice dull. “I guess I don’t. I’ll contact Hough and hope for the best.”

“Meanwhile,” Kelgarries spoke briskly, “we’ll do what we can to speed up the program as it now stands. I suggest you take off for New York within the hour﻿—”

“Me? Why?” Ashe asked with a trace of suspicion.

“Because I can’t leave without acting directly against orders, and that would put us wrong immediately. You see Hough and talk to him personally﻿—put it to him straight. He’ll have to have all the facts if he’s going to counter any move from Stanton before the council. You know every argument we can use and all the proof on our side, and you’re authority enough to make it count.”

===Chinese===
「不，」他沉沉地答道，嗓音黯哑，「我想我不是当真的。我去联系霍夫（Hough），往好处盼吧。」

「同时，」凯尔加里斯干脆利落地说，「我们尽力把现行计划往前赶。我建议你一小时内动身去纽约——」

「我？为什么？」阿什问，带一丝疑色。

「因为我一走就是公然抗命，那会立刻让我们陷入被动。你去见霍夫，当面跟他谈——把话挑明了说。他要在理事会上应对斯坦顿的任何动作，就必须掌握全部事实。我们拿得出手的每一个论点、我方的全部证据，你都了然于胸，而且你的权威足够让这些话有分量。」

===Original===
“If I can do all that, I will.” Ashe was alert and eager. The colonel, seeing his change of expression, felt easier.

But Kelgarries stood a moment watching Ashe as he hurried down a side corridor, before he moved on slowly to his own box of office. Once inside he sat for a long unhappy time staring at the wall and seeing nothing but the pictures produced by his thoughts. Then he pressed a button and read off the symbols which flashed on a small visa-screen set in his desk. Another button pushed, and he picked up a hand mike to relay an order which might postpone trouble for a while. Ashe was far too valuable a man to lose, and his emotions could boil him straight into disaster over this.

“Bidwell﻿—reschedule Team A. They are to go to the Hypno-Lab instead of the reserve in ten minutes.”

Releasing the mike, he again stared at the wall. No one dared interrupt a hypno-training period, and this one would last three hours. Ashe could not possibly see the trainees before he left for New York. And that would remove one temptation from his path﻿—he would not talk at the wrong time.

===Chinese===
「只要我做得到，我一定办到。」阿什振作而热切。上校看他神色一变，心里也松快了些。

凯尔加里斯站了片刻，望着阿什匆匆拐进一条侧廊，才缓步走向自己那间盒子似的办公室。一进门，他便郁郁地枯坐良久，瞪着墙壁，眼前晃的尽是思绪投射出的图景。而后他按下按钮，读出嵌在桌面的小显像屏（visa-screen）上闪现的符号。再按一个按钮，他拿起手持话筒，传达了一道也许能把麻烦推迟一阵的命令。阿什这个人太宝贵，失不得；而这一回，他的情绪足以把他直接推进灾祸里去。

「比德维尔（Bidwell）——改排A队的日程。十分钟后他们去催眠实验室（Hypno-Lab），不去预备室。」

放下话筒，他又瞪起了墙壁。催眠训练期间没人敢打扰，这一轮要持续三个钟头。阿什动身去纽约之前，无论如何见不着受训队员了。这样就除掉了路上的一个诱惑——他不会在不当说话的时候说话了。

===Original===
Kelgarries’ mouth twisted sourly. He had no pride in what he was doing. And he was perfectly certain that Ruthven would win and that Ashe’s fears of Redax were well founded. It all came back to the old basic tenet of the service: the end justified the means. They must use every method and man under their control to make sure that Topaz would remain a western possession, even though that strange planet now swung far beyond the sky which covered both the western and eastern alliances on Terra. Time had run out too fast; they were being forced to play what cards they held, even though those might be very low ones. Ashe would be back, but not, Kelgarries hoped, until this had been decided one way or another. Not until this was finished.

Finished! Kelgarries blinked at the wall. Perhaps *they* were finished, too. No one would know until the transport ship landed on that other world which appeared on the direction tape symbolized by a jewellike disk of gold-brown which had given it the code name of Topaz.

===Chinese===
凯尔加里斯的嘴角泛起苦涩的扭曲。他对自己正在做的事毫无得意可言。而且他完全确定：鲁思文会赢，阿什对雷达克斯的忧惧也确有根据。说到底，还是这一行的老信条：目的可为手段正名。他们必须动用手底下的每一分方法与人力，确保托帕兹仍是西方的一份产业，纵然那颗奇异的行星如今已远悬于罩着地球东西两大联盟的天空之外。时间耗得太快；他们被迫把手里的牌尽数打出，哪怕那些牌面低得可怜。阿什会回来的——但凯尔加里斯希望，别在这件事定局之前回来。别在这件事了结之前。

了结！凯尔加里斯对着墙壁眨了眨眼。也许*他们*也一起了结了呢。答案要等运输星船降落那个世界方能揭晓——在航向磁带上，它由一枚金褐色宝石般的圆盘标示，也由此得了「托帕兹」这个代号。

## II / II

===Original===
There were an even dozen of the airborne guardians, each following the swing of its own orbital path just within the atmospheric envelope of the planet which glowed as a great bronze-golden gem in the four-world system of a yellow star. The globes had been launched to form a web of protection around Topaz six months earlier, and the highest skill had gone into their production. Just as contact mines sown in a harbor could close that landfall to ships not knowing the secret channel, so was this world supposedly closed to any spaceship not equipped with the signal to ward off the sphere missiles.

That was the theory of the new off-world settlers whose protection they were to be, already tested as well as possible, but as yet not put to the ultimate proof. The small bright globes spun undisturbed across a two-mooned sky at night and made reassuring blips on an installation screen by day.

Then a thirteenth object winked into being, began the encircling, closing spiral of descent. A sphere resembling the warden-globes, it was a hundred times their size, and its orbit was purposefully controlled by instruments under the eye and hand of a human pilot.

===Chinese===
空中守卫不多不少整整一打，每一颗都沿着各自的轨道弧线运行，紧贴着行星大气层的内缘；那颗行星在黄色恒星的四行星系统里，如一枚青铜金色的巨大宝石熠熠生辉。这些球体于六个月前发射升空，在托帕兹四周织成一张防护网，制造它们时倾注了最顶尖的工艺。正如敷设在海港里的触发水雷能把那处泊地对不知晓秘密航道的外来船只封闭，这个世界据信同样对任何飞船封闭——除非它携有能让球形导弹退避的信号。

这便是它们所要保护的那些新来异星殖民者的理论，已尽可能检验过，却尚未经受终极的证明。小小的亮球夜间在双月同悬的天穹下安然旋转，白天则在基地装置的屏幕上打出令人安心的光点。

而后，第十三个物体倏然亮起，开始一圈圈收拢的螺旋下降。那球体与警戒球（warden-globe）形似，个头却有它们百倍之大，它的轨道由仪器刻意操控，而仪器又听命于一位人类飞行员的眼睛与手。

===Original===
Four men were strapped down on cushioned sling-seats in the control cabin of the Western Alliance ship, two hanging where their fingers might reach buttons and levers, the others merely passengers, their own labor waiting for the time when they would set down on the alien soil of Topaz. The planet hung there in their visa-screen, richly beautiful in its amber gold, growing larger, nearer, so that they could pick out features of seas, continents, mountain ranges, which had been studied on tape until they were familiar, yet now were strangely unfamiliar too.

One of the warden-globes alerted, oscillated in its set path, whirled faster as its delicate interior mechanisms responded to the awakening spark which would send it on its mission of destruction. A relay clicked, but for the smallest fraction of a millimeter failed to set the proper course. On the instrument, far below, which checked the globe’s new course the mistake was not noted.

The screen of the ship spiraling toward Topaz registered a path which would bring it into violent contact with the globe. They were still some hundreds of miles apart when the alarm rang. The pilot’s hand clawed out at the bank of controls; under the almost intolerable pressure of their descent, there was so little he could do. His crooked fingers fell back powerlessly from the buttons and levers; his mouth was a twisted grimace of bleak acceptance as the beat of the signal increased.

===Chinese===
西方联盟飞船的操纵舱里，四个人被束带固定在带衬垫的吊座上：两人悬坐在指尖够得着按钮与操纵杆的位置，另两人只是乘客，他们自己的活计要等到踏上托帕兹异星土壤之时才开场。行星悬在他们的显像屏里，琥珀金色，华彩丰盈，越来越大、越来越近，认得出海洋、大陆、山脉的轮廓——那些地貌在磁带上早已研读得烂熟，此刻却又有种说不出的陌生。

一颗警戒球警觉起来，在既定轨道上震荡，转速加快——它精密的内部机件响应了那簇苏醒的电火花，即将把它送上毁灭的使命。一具继电器咔嗒一响，却以不到一毫米的毫厘之差，没能校准航向。地面上远处那台负责核查这颗球新航向的仪器，没有把这个差错记下。

正螺旋逼近托帕兹的飞船，屏幕上显示出一条将与那颗警戒球猛烈相撞的航线。警报拉响时，两者还相距数百英里。飞行员的手朝控制台抓去；在几乎难以承受的下降过载之下，他能做的微乎其微。扭曲的手指无力地从按钮和操纵杆上垂落；信号节拍越来越急，他的嘴扭成一个听天由命的惨淡怪相。

===Original===
One of the passengers forced his head around on the padded rest, fought to form words, to speak to his companion. The other was staring ahead at the screen, his thick lips wide and flat against his teeth in a snarl of rage.

“They﻿ ﻿… are﻿ ﻿… here﻿ ﻿…”

Ruthven paid no attention to the obvious as stated by his fellow scientist. His fury was a red, pulsing thing inside him, fed by his own helplessness. To be pinned here so near his goal, fastened up as a target for an inanimate but cunningly fashioned weapon, ate into him like a stream of deadly acid. His big gamble would puff out in a blast of fire to light up Topaz’s sky, with nothing left﻿—nothing. On the armrest of his sling-seat his nails scratched deep.

===Chinese===
一名乘客在衬垫头枕上艰难地扭过头，挣扎着吐字，想对同伴说话。另一个则直瞪着前方的屏幕，厚嘴唇绷平贴着牙齿，成了一副暴怒的狞相。

「他们……来……了……」

同僚科学家道出的这番显而易见的事实，鲁思文根本无心理会。他的怒火在体内烧成一团搏动的红，全由他自身的无能为力喂养着。眼看目标近在咫尺却被钉死在此，被固定成一枚靶子，等着一件没有生命却造得巧夺天工的武器——这像一道致命的酸液在他体内咬噬。他的一场豪赌将在一团烈焰中化作照亮托帕兹天空的烟花，什么都留不下——什么都没有。他的指甲在吊座扶手上抠出深深的划痕。

===Original===
The four men in the control cabin could only sit and watch, waiting for the rendezvous which would blot them out. Ruthven’s flaming anger was a futile blaze. His companion in the passenger seat had closed his eyes, his lips moving soundlessly in an expression of his own scattered thoughts. The pilot and his assistant divided their attention between the screen, with its appalling message, and the controls they could not effectively use, feverishly seeking a way out in these last moments.

Below them in the bowl of the ship were those who would not know the end consciously﻿—save in one compartment. In a padded cage a prick-eared head stirred where it rested on forepaws, slitted eyes blinked, aware not only of familiar surroundings, but also of the tension and fear generated by human minds and emotions levels above. A pointed nose raised, and there was a growling deep in a throat covered with thick buff-gray hair.

===Chinese===
操纵舱里的四个人只能坐着干看，等待那场把他们一笔抹去的会合。鲁思文熊熊的怒火只是徒劳的燃烧。客座上的同伴闭起了眼睛，嘴唇无声地翕动，泄露着他纷乱四散的思绪。飞行员和助手把注意力分给传出凶讯的屏幕与形同虚设的操纵台，在最后的时刻里发狂似的寻觅生路。

在他们下方、飞船的碗形舱段里，是那些将无从在有意识的状态下知晓结局的人——除了一间隔舱之外。铺衬垫的笼中，一只尖耳竖立的头颅从前爪上抬动，眯缝的眼眨了眨；它察觉到的不止是熟悉的环境，还有上面几层人类心智与情绪所激起的紧张与恐惧。尖吻扬起，覆着厚厚沙黄灰毛的喉咙深处滚出一声低吼。

===Original===
The growl aroused another similar captive. Knowing yellow eyes met yellow eyes. An intelligence, which was certainly not that of the animal body which contained it, fought down instinct raging to send both those bodies hurtling at the fastenings of the twin cages. Curiosity and the ability to adapt had been bred into both from time immemorial. Then something else had been added to sly and cunning brains. A step up had been taken﻿—to weld intelligence to cunning, connect thought to instinct.

More than a generation earlier mankind had chosen barren desert﻿—the “white sands” of New Mexico﻿—as a testing ground for atomic experiments. Humankind could be barred, warded out of the radiation limits; the natural desert dwellers, four-footed and winged, could not be so controlled.

For thousands of years, since the first southward roving Amerindian tribes had met with their kind, there had been a hunter of the open country, a smaller cousin of the wolf, whose natural abilities had made an undeniable impression on the human mind. He was in countless Indian legends as the Shaper or the Trickster, sometimes friend, sometimes enemy. Godling for some tribes, father of all evil for others. In the wealth of tales the coyote, above all other animals, had a firm place.

===Chinese===
低吼惊醒了另一只同样的囚徒。两双慧黠的黄眼睛对上了眼。一种智性——绝非承载它的兽躯本身该有的智性——压下了咆哮的本能；那本能催促着两具躯体朝并排双笼的锁扣猛冲。好奇与应变之力，自洪荒以来就烙印在它们的天性里。而后，那两颗狡黠机诈的脑袋里又被添进了别的东西。一步跃升已然完成——把智力焊上狡诈，让思维接通本能。

早在一代人之前，人类就选中了荒漠——新墨西哥州的「白沙」——作为原子试验的场址。人类可以被拦住、被隔离在辐射界线之外；沙漠里的原住民，四足的和带翅的，却无法这般管控。

数千年来，自第一批南迁漫游的美洲印第安部落与他们相遇起，旷野上就有这么一位猎手——狼的小个子堂亲，其天生本领在人类心智上烙下了不容否认的印记。在数不清的印第安传说里，他是「造物者」（Shaper），又是「促狭鬼」（Trickster），时而是朋友，时而是仇敌。有些部落奉他为小神，另一些部落骂他是万恶之源。在传说的宝库中，郊狼高于一切兽类，占有一个稳固的位置。

===Original===
Driven by the press of civilization into the badlands and deserts, fought with poison, gun, and trap, the coyote had survived, adapting to new ways with all his legendary cunning. Those who had reviled him as vermin had unwillingly added to the folklore which surrounded him, telling their own tales of robbed traps, skillful escapes. He continued to be a trickster, laughing on moonlit nights from the tops of ridges at those who would hunt him down.

Then, close to the end of the twentieth century, when myths were scoffed at, the stories of the coyote’s slyness began once more on a fantastic scale. And finally scientists were sufficiently intrigued to seek out this creature that seemed to display in truth all the abilities credited to his immortal namesake by pre-Columbian tribes.

What they discovered was indeed shattering to certain closed minds. For the coyote had not only adapted to the country of the white sands; he had evolved into something which could not be dismissed as an animal, clever and cunning, but limited to beast range. Six cubs had been brought back on the first expedition, coyote in body, their developing minds different. The grandchildren of those cubs were now in the ship’s cages, their mutated senses alert, ready for the slightest chance of escape. Sent to Topaz as eyes and ears for less keenly endowed humans, they were not completely under the domination of man. The range of their mental powers was still uncomprehended by those who had bred, trained, and worked with them from the days their eyes had opened and they had taken their first wobbly steps away from their dams.

===Chinese===
在文明的重压下被驱入荒地和沙漠，遭毒药、枪械和陷阱围剿，郊狼却活了下来，以它那传奇般的狡黠应对着新的生存之道。那些咒它是害兽的人，反倒不情愿地为环绕它的民间传说添了砖，讲起陷阱被劫、巧妙脱身的故事。它依旧做它的促狭鬼，在月夜里立于山脊之巅大笑，嘲弄那些想要赶尽杀绝的人。

及至二十世纪末，神话早已被人嗤之以鼻，郊狼的狡黠却再度被传得神乎其神。终于，科学家们被勾起了足够的好奇，去寻访这种生灵——它似乎真真切切地展现出前哥伦布时代各部落加诸它那位不朽同名传奇身上的一切本事。

他们的发现，对某些闭塞的头脑而言确实石破天惊。因为郊狼不单适应了白沙滩上的国度；它已经进化成一种不能等闲视为走兽的东西——固然聪明狡诈，却已不受兽类范围的局限。第一支考察队带回了六只幼崽，躯体是郊狼，发育中的心智却与众不同。如今飞船笼中的，正是那些幼崽的孙辈；它们变异的感官警觉万分，只等一丝半毫的脱逃之机。它们被送往托帕兹，给感官远不如它们敏锐的人类充当耳目，却并不完全受人的支配。它们心智能力的边界，就连那些从它们睁眼、初次离开母兽颤巍巍迈步之日起便饲养、训练、与之共事的人，也始终摸不透。

===Original===
The male growled again, his lips wrinkling back in a snarl as the emanations of fear from the men he could not see reached panic peak. He still crouched, belly flat, on the protecting pads of his cage; but he strove now to wriggle closer to the door, just as his mate made the same effort.

Between the animals and those in the control cabin lay the others﻿—forty of them. Their bodies were cushioned and protected with every ingenious device known to those who had placed them there so many weeks earlier. Their minds were free of the ship, roving into places where men had not trod before, a territory potentially more dangerous than any solid earth could ever be.

Operation Retrograde had returned men bodily into the past, sending agents to hunt mammoths, follow the roads of the Bronze Age traders, ride with Attila and Genghis Khan, pull bows among the archers of ancient Egypt. But Redax returned men in mind to the paths of their ancestors, or this was the theory. And those who slept here and now in their narrow boxes, lay under its government, while the men who had arbitrarily set them so could only assume they were actually reliving the lives of Apache nomads in the wide southwestern wastes of the late eighteenth and early nineteenth centuries.

===Chinese===
雄郊狼又低吼一声，双唇皱起、龇成怒吼之状——那些它看不见的人类发散出的恐惧，已经攀上了惊惶的顶点。它仍肚皮贴地伏在笼中的护垫上，此刻却拼命朝笼门蹭近，它的伴侣也在做着同样的努力。

在兽笼与操纵舱之间，躺着其余的人——整整四十个。数周前把他们安置于此的人，动用了一切巧妙的装置来衬垫、保护他们的身躯。他们的心智却脱开了飞船，漫游到人类未曾涉足之地——那片疆域潜藏的凶险，远非任何实土大地可比。

逆行行动曾让人的肉身返回过去，派特工去猎猛犸、循青铜时代商旅的道路、与阿提拉和成吉思汗并辔驰骋、在古埃及弓手的行列里张弓放箭。而雷达克斯是把人的心智送回祖先走过的老路——至少理论如此。此刻在狭窄卧箱里沉睡的这些人，正受着它的管辖；而那些专断地把他们弄到这步田地的人，也只能推想他们确实正在重历十八世纪末、十九世纪初阿帕奇游牧民在西南广袤荒原上的生活。

===Original===
Above, the pilot’s hand pushed out again, fighting the pressure to reach one particular button. That, too, had been a last-minute addition, an experiment which had only had partial testing. To use it was the final move he could make, and he was already half convinced of its uselessness.

With no faith and only a very wan hope, he sent that round of metal flush with the board. What followed no one ever lived to explain.

On the planet the installation which tracked the missiles flashed on a screen bright enough to blind momentarily the duty man on watch, and its tracker was shaken off course. When it jiggled back into line it was no longer the efficient eye-in-the-sky it had been, though its tenders were not to realize that for an important minute or two.

===Chinese===
上层，飞行员的手又一次顶着重压推出去，去够某个特定的按钮。那也是临起飞前才添上的东西，一项只做过部分试验的尝试。按下它是他能走的最后一着棋，而他已经半信这一着毫无用处。

怀着零点的信心和一线惨淡的希望，他把那枚圆形金属钮按得与面板齐平。随后发生的事，再没有一个活人能活着解释。

行星表面上，追踪导弹的那套装置在屏幕上爆出一片强光，晃得值班员一时目不能视，追踪器也被震离了准线。待它抖回原位时，它已不再是先前那双高效的天眼——只是操作人员在一两分钟的关键时刻里浑然不觉。

===Original===
While the ship, now out of control, sped in dizzy whirls toward Topaz, engines fought blindly to stabilize, to reestablish their functions. Some succeeded, some wobbled in and out of the danger zone, two failed. And in the control cabin three dead men spun in prisoning seats.

Dr. James Ruthven, blood bubbling from his lips with every shallow breath he could draw, fought the stealthy tide of blackness which crept up his brain, his stubborn will holding to rags of consciousness, refusing to acknowledge the pain of his fatally injured body.

The orbiting ship was on an erratic path. Slowly the machines were correcting, relays clicking, striving to bring it to a landing under autopilot. All the ingenuity built into a mechanical brain was now centered in landing the globe.

===Chinese===
失控的飞船天旋地转地扑向托帕兹，发动机盲目挣扎着要稳住、要恢复功能。有的成功了，有的在危险边缘摇摇欲坠，两台彻底罢工。操纵舱里，三具尸体在囚笼般的座椅中打着转。

詹姆斯·鲁思文医生每吸进一口浅息，唇边便涌起一串血泡；他与悄悄漫上头脑的黑暗潮水搏斗，倔强的意志死死抓住意识的碎片残缕，拒绝承认那具伤重必死的躯体传来的痛楚。

盘旋下降的飞船航线散乱。机器在缓缓修正，继电器咔嗒作响，竭力要让自动驾驶完成降落。机械脑里蕴藏的全部巧思，此刻都集于一事：让这颗球体落到地面。

===Original===
It was not a good landing, in fact a very bad one, for the sphere touched a mountain side, scraped down rocks, shearing away a portion of its outer bulk. But the mountain barrier was now between it and the base from which the missiles had been launched, and the crash had not been recorded on that tracking instrument. So far as the watchers several hundred miles away knew, the warden in the sky had performed as promised. Their first line of defense had proven satisfactory, and there had been no unauthorized landing on Topaz.

In the wreckage of the control cabin Ruthven pawed at the fastenings of his sling-chair. He no longer tried to suppress the moans every effort tore out of him. Time held the whip, drove him. He rolled from his seat to the floor, lay there gasping, as again he fought doggedly to remain above the waves﻿—those frightening, fast-coming waves of dark faintness.

===Chinese===
这不是一次好的降落，实际上糟透了：球体擦上山坡，剐着岩石滑下，外层结构被削掉了一块。但此刻山体屏障隔在了它与导弹发射基地之间，坠机未被那台追踪仪器记录在案。在几百英里外的监视者们看来，天上的守卫不辱使命。他们的第一道防线被证明运转良好，托帕兹没有遭到任何未经许可的降落。

在操纵舱的残骸中，鲁思文扒抓着吊座的锁扣。每一分力气都从他喉咙里撕出一声呻吟，他已不再试图压抑。时间执鞭驱赶着他。他从座椅滚到地板，躺在那里喘息，再一次顽强搏斗，只为浮在浪头之上——那些骇人的、飞快扑来的昏黑浪头。

===Original===
Somehow he was crawling, crawling along a tilted surface until he gained the well where the ladder to the lower section hung, now at an acute angle. It was that angle which helped him to the next level.

He was too dazed to realize the meaning of the crumpled bulkheads. There was a spur of bare rock under his hands as he edged over and around twisted metal. The moans were now a gobbling, burbling, almost continuous cry as he reached his goal﻿—a small cabin still intact.

For long moments of anguish he paused by the chair there, afraid that he could not make the last effort, raise his almost inert bulk up to the point where he could reach the Redax release. For a second of unusual clarity he wondered if there was any reason for this supreme ordeal, whether any of the sleepers could be aroused. This might now be a ship of the dead.

===Chinese===
不知怎么的，他爬啊爬，沿着一面倾斜的壁面挪到了竖井口——通往下层的梯子悬在那儿，如今斜成陡角。恰恰是那个斜度，助他下到了下一层。

他昏沉得辨不出那些坍皱舱壁意味着什么。他翻过绕过扭曲的金属，掌下是一处裸岩的尖突。呻吟如今成了含混咕哝、几乎连绵不绝的哀号，他终于抵达目的地——一间仍然完好无损的小舱。

他在那椅子旁苦挨了许久，生怕自己使不出最后的力气，把这具近乎不听使唤的身躯挪到够得着雷达克斯释放开关的地方。难得有一秒钟的清明，他暗想这番极刑般的煎熬究竟还有没有意义，沉睡者中还有没有一个能被唤醒。此刻这也许已是一艘死人之船。

===Original===
His right hand, his arm, and finally his bulk over the seat, he braced himself and brought his left hand up. He could not use any of the fingers; it was like lifting numb, heavy weights. But he lurched forward, swept the unfeeling lump of cold flesh down against the release in a gesture which he knew must be his final move. And, as he fell back to the floor, Dr. Ruthven could not be certain whether he had succeeded or failed. He tried to screw his head around, to focus his eyes upward at that switch. Was it down or still stubbornly up, locking the sleepers into confinement? But there was a fog between; he could not see it﻿—or anything.

The light in the cabin flickered, was gone as another circuit in the broken ship failed. It was dark, too, in the small cubby below which housed the two cages. Chance, which had snuffed out nineteen lives in the space globe, had missed ripping open that cabin on the mountain side. Five yards down the corridor the outside fabric of the ship was split wide open, the crisp air native to Topaz entering, sending a message to two keen noses through the combination of odors now pervading the wreckage.

And the male coyote went into action. Days ago he had managed to work loose the lower end of the mesh which fronted his cage, but his mind had told him that a sortie inside the ship was valueless. The odd rapport he’d had with the human brains, unknown to them, had operated to keep him to the old role of cunning deception, which in the past had saved countless of his species from sudden and violent death. Now with teeth and paws he went diligently to work, urged on by the whines of his mate, that tantalizing smell of an outside world tickling their nostrils﻿—a wild world, lacking the taint of man-places.

===Chinese===
右手、右臂，最后连整个身子都压上座椅，他撑稳了自己，抬起左手。手指一根也使唤不动，就像在提一串麻木的重物。可他仍旧向前扑去，把那团失去知觉的冷肉横扫下来砸向释放开关——他明白，这一下必是他此生最后一搏。跌回地板上时，鲁思文医生已无法断定自己成了还是败了。他竭力拧过脖颈，想让眼睛对焦在那具开关上。它是扳下去了，还是仍在倔强地立着，把沉睡者们锁在禁锢之中？可中间隔着一层雾；他看不见它——也看不见任何东西。

小舱里的灯光闪烁了几下，随着破船又一处电路失效而熄灭。下面那间安置双笼的小隔舱里，同样一片漆黑。命运之神在这颗太空球舱里掐灭了十九条人命，却偏偏放过了贴在山壁上的这间小舱。沿走廊往下五码，飞船的外壳豁开一道大口，托帕兹本土的凛冽空气灌了进来，携着弥漫残骸的种种气味，给两只敏锐的鼻子捎去了讯息。

雄郊狼行动起来了。几天前它就弄松了笼前网格的下端，但它的心智告诉它，在飞船内部出击毫无价值。它与那些人类头脑之间的奇妙感应——人类自己毫不知情——一直促使它守着狡诈示弱的老戏码；正是这一手，过去曾让它无数同类躲过暴毙的横祸。此刻它用牙齿和爪子勤勤恳恳地干起活来，伴侣的呜咽催着它，外面世界那撩人的气味挠着它们的鼻尖——一个蛮荒世界，没有人类居所的浊气。

===Original===
He slipped under the loosened mesh and stood up to paw at the front of the female’s cage. One forepaw caught in the latch and pressed it down, and the weight of the door swung against him. Together they were free now to reach the corridor and see ahead the subdued light of a strange moon beckoning them on into the open.

The female, always more cautious than her mate, lingered behind as he trotted forward, his ears a-prick with curiosity. Their training had been the same since cubhood﻿—to range and explore, but always in the company and at the order of man. This was not according to the pattern she knew, and she was suspicious. But to her sensitive nose the smell of the ship was an offense, and the puffs of breeze from without enticing. Her mate had already slipped through the break; now he barked with excitement and wonder, and she trotted on to join him.

Above, the Redax, which had never been intended to stand rough usage, proved to be a better survivor of the crash than most of the other installations. Power purred along a network of lines, activated beams, turned off and on a series of fixtures in those coffin-beds. For five of the sleepers﻿—nothing. The cabin which had held them was a flattened smear against the mountain side. Three more half aroused, choked, fought for life and breath in a darkness which was a mercifully short nightmare, and succumbed.

===Chinese===
它从松脱的网格下钻出，站起来去扒雌笼的前壁。一只前爪勾进门闩，压了下去，笼门的自重顺势朝它荡开。这下它们双双自由了，可以走进走廊，望见前方一轮陌生月亮的幽光，正召唤它们奔向旷野。

雄郊狼小跑向前、双耳因好奇竖得笔直时，素来比它谨慎的雌郊狼却落在后面磨蹭。自幼崽时起，它们受的训练如出一辙——巡查与探察，但永远要与人相伴、听人号令。眼前这一幕不合她熟识的章法，她心存戒备。可对她那敏感的鼻子而言，飞船的气味是一种冒犯，外头阵阵拂来的微风却是诱饵。她的伴侣已经钻出了豁口，正兴奋而惊奇地吠叫；她便也小跑着赶去会合。

上层，那台从没打算经受粗暴对待的雷达克斯，在这场坠机中反倒比大多数设备更能幸存。电流在线路网络中低吟，激活光束，把那一列棺床（coffin-bed）上的装置一次次开合。对其中五名沉睡者而言——一切归于虚无。容纳他们的那间舱室已贴着山壁压成一片扁平的残迹。另有三人半醒过来，窒息着，在黑暗中为性命和呼吸挣扎——幸而那场噩梦短促——随即断了气。

===Original===
But in the cabin nearest the rent through which the coyotes had escaped, a young man sat up abruptly, looking into the dark with wide-open, terror-haunted eyes. He clawed for purchase against the smooth edge of the box in which he had lain, somehow got to his knees, weaving weakly back and forth, and half fell, half pushed to the floor where he could stand only by keeping his hold on the box.

Dazed, sick, weak, he swayed there, aware only of himself and his own sensations. There were small sounds in the dark, a stilled moan, a gasping sigh. But that meant nothing. Within him grew a compulsion to be out of this place, his terror making him lurch forward.

His flailing hand rapped painfully against an upright surface which his questing fingers identified hazily as an exit. Unconsciously he fumbled along the surface of the door until it gave under that weak pressure. Then he was out, his head swimming, drawn by the light behind the wall rent.

He progressed toward that in a scrambling crawl, making his way over the splintered skin of the globe. Then he dropped with a jarring thud onto the mound of earth the ship had pushed before it during its downward slide. Limply he tumbled on in a small cascade of clods and sand, hitting against a less movable rock with force enough to roll him over on his back and stun him again.

===Chinese===
可就在离郊狼逃出那道裂口最近的舱室里，一个年轻人猛然坐起，睁大惊惶失色的眼睛望进黑暗。他抠住卧箱光滑的边沿借力，不知怎么跪了起来，身子虚弱地摇摇晃晃，半跌半撑地落到地板上——站着，全凭死死抓住那只箱子。

晕眩、作呕、虚弱，他摇摇晃晃地立在那里，意识里只剩自己和自己的感觉。黑暗中有细碎的声响，一声戛然而止的呻吟，一声抽气的叹息。可这些都毫无意义。他心里长出一股强迫的冲动——离开这个地方，恐惧推搡着他踉跄向前。

他胡乱挥舞的手痛磕在一面竖立的壁板上，摸索的手指昏昏然辨认出那是一扇出口。他下意识地顺着门板摸索，直到门在那点微弱的力道下让开了。于是他出来了，头昏眼花，被墙壁裂口后面的光牵动着。

他手脚并用地朝那光亮爬去，翻过球体崩裂的外皮，随即砰的一声磕落在土丘上——那是飞船下滑时推挤起的土堆。他又软塌塌地跟着一小股土块沙粒翻滚而下，撞上一块纹丝不动的岩石，力道之猛，把他掀了个仰面朝天，再度撞得昏死过去。

===Original===
The second and smaller moon of Topaz swung brightly through the sky, its weird green rays making the blood-streaked face of the explorer an alien mask. It had passed well on to the horizon, and its large yellow companion had risen when a yapping broke the small sounds of the night.

As the *yipp, yipp, yipp* arose in a crescendo, the man stirred, putting one hand to his head. His eyes opened, he looked vaguely about him and sat up. Behind him was the torn and ripped ship, but he did not look back at it.

Instead, he got to his feet and staggered out into the direct path of the moonlight. Inside his brain there was a whirl of thoughts, memories, emotions. Perhaps Ruthven or one of his assistants could have explained that chaotic mixture for what it was. But for all practical purposes Travis Fox﻿—Amerindian Time Agent, member of Team A, Operation Cochise﻿—was far less of a thinking animal now than the two coyotes paying their ritual addresses to a moon which was not the one of their vanished homeland.

===Chinese===
托帕兹那第二颗较小的月亮明亮地划过天际，诡异的绿辉把探险家血痕纵横的脸照成一副异星面具。待它行至天边、那颗较大的黄色伙伴升起时，一阵吠鸣划破了夜晚的细碎声响。

随着*吠、吠、吠*的短音一阵高过一阵，那人动了动，一只手扶上脑袋。他睁开眼，茫然四顾，坐了起来。身后就是那艘撕裂破碎的飞船，他却没有回头看它一眼。

他反倒站起身，踉跄着走进月亮直泻的光路。他的脑中旋搅着乱麻般的思绪、记忆与情绪。换作鲁思文或他的哪位助手，也许还能解释清这团混沌的来龙去脉。但就实而论，特拉维斯·福克斯——美洲印第安裔时间代理人、科奇西行动A队队员——此刻这头「会思考的动物」，可比那两只郊狼逊色远了；它们正对着一轮月亮行它们惯常的礼数，尽管那月亮并非它们已然消失的故土之月。

===Original===
Travis wavered on, drawn somehow by that howling. It was familiar, a thread of something real through all the broken clutter in his head. He stumbled, fell, crawled up again, but he kept on.

Above, the female coyote lowered her head, drew a test sniff of a new scent. She recognized that as part of the proper way of life. She yapped once at her mate, but he was absorbed in his night song, his muzzle pointed moonward as he voiced a fine wailing.

Travis tripped, pitched forward on his hands and knees, and felt the jar of such a landing shoot up his stiffened forearms. He tried to get up, but his body only twisted, so he landed on his back and lay looking up at the moon.

A strong, familiar odor﻿ ﻿… then a shadow looming above him. Hot breath against his cheek, and the swift sweep of an animal tongue on his face. He flung up his hand, gripped thick fur, and held on as if he had found one anchor of sanity in a world gone completely mad.

===Chinese===
特拉维斯晃晃悠悠地往前挪，冥冥中被那阵嗥叫牵引着。那声音是熟悉的，是他脑中满目狼藉里的一缕真实。他绊倒，跌翻，又爬起来，却始终没有停。

高处，雌郊狼低下头，试探地嗅了嗅一股新的气味。她认出那是正当生活方式的一部分。她朝伴侣短吠了一声，可它已沉醉在夜歌之中，吻尖直指月亮，喉间放出一串悠长的哀鸣。

特拉维斯绊了一下，向前扑成手脚着地，着地的震劲顺着他僵直的前臂窜上来。他想爬起来，身子却只是拧了一下，结果仰面摔倒，躺在那里仰望月亮。

一股浓烈而熟悉的气味……随后一片阴影罩上他。热气拂上脸颊，一条兽舌飞快地舔过他的脸。他猛地扬手，攥住厚厚的毛皮，死死抓住不放，仿佛在这个彻底疯掉的世界上，他终于抓住了一枚仅存的清醒之锚。

## III / III

===Original===
Travis, one knee braced against the red earth, blinked as he parted a screen of tall rust-brown grass with cautious fingers to look out into a valley where golden mist clouded most of the landscape. His head ached with dull persistence, the pain fostered in some way by his own bewilderment. To study the land ahead was like trying to see through one picture interposed over another and far different one. He knew what ought to be there, but what was before him was very dissimilar.

A buff-gray shape flitted through the tall cover grass, and Travis tensed. *Mba’a*﻿—coyote? Or were these companions of his actually *ga-n*, spirits who could choose their shape at will and had, oddly, this time assumed the bodies of man’s tricky enemy? Were they *ndendai*﻿—enemies﻿—or *dalaanbiyat’i*, allies? In this mad world he did not know.

*Ei’dik’e?* His mind formed a word he did not speak: Friend?

===Chinese===
特拉维斯单膝抵着红土，眨了眨眼，用小心翼翼的手指拨开一道高高的铁锈色草幕，望向山谷——金色雾霭笼住了那里的大部分景物。他的头持续隐隐作痛，那痛楚不知怎的被他的茫然无措滋养着。端详眼前的原野，就像努力看透一张叠印在另一张截然不同画面上的图画。他知道那里本该是什么模样，可眼前的景象却大相径庭。

一道沙黄灰的身影掠过高高的掩护草丛，特拉维斯绷紧了。*Mba’a*（郊狼）？还是说，他这两位同伴其实是*ga-n*（山灵）——那些能随意择形化身的精怪，这一回偏偏怪里怪气地披上了人类那诡诈宿敌的躯壳？他们是*ndendai*（敌人），还是*dalaanbiyat’i*（盟友）？在这个疯狂的世界里，他无从知晓。

*Ei’dik’e*（朋友）？他脑中浮出一个没有出口的词：朋友？

===Original===
Yellow eyes met his directly. Dimly he had been aware, ever since awaking in this strange wilderness with the coming of morning light, that the four-footed ones trotting with him as he walked aimlessly had unbeastlike traits. Not only did they face him eye-to-eye, but in some ways they appeared able to read his thoughts.

He had longed for water to ease the burning in his throat, the ever-present pain in his head, and the creatures had nudged him in another direction, bringing him to a pool where he had mouthed liquid with a strange sweet, but not unpleasant taste.

Now he had given them names, names which had come out of the welter of dreams which shadowed his stumbling journey across this weird country.

===Chinese===
一双黄眼睛直直迎上他的目光。隐隐地，他早已察觉——自晨光初照时在这片陌生荒野中醒来起——那两只随着他漫无目的地行走、一路小跑的四足伙伴，有着不像走兽的品性。它们不但与他四目相对，而且在某种意义上，似乎能读懂他的心思。

他曾渴盼清水，好浇一浇喉咙里的灼烧、头脑中无时不在的疼痛；那两个生灵便用鼻尖把他拱向另一个方向，引到一泓水潭边，他啜饮的液体带着一种奇异的甜味，却并不难喝。

如今他已给它们起了名字——名字是从那团梦的乱流里浮出来的，正是那乱梦，笼着他跌跌撞撞横穿这片奇土的旅程。

===Original===
Nalik’ideyu (Maiden-Who-Walks-Ridges) was the female who continued to shepherd him along, never venturing too far from his side. Naginlta (He-Who-Scouts-Ahead) was the male who did just that, disappearing at long intervals and then returning to face the man and his mate as if conveying some report necessary to their journey.

It was Nalik’ideyu who sought out Travis now, her red tongue lolling from her mouth as she panted. Not from exertion, he was certain of that. No, she was excited and eager﻿ ﻿… on the hunt! That was it﻿—a hunt!

Travis’ own tongue ran across his lips as an impression hit him with feral force. There was meat﻿—rich, fresh﻿—just ahead. Meat that lived, waiting to be killed. Inside him his own avid hunger roused, shaking him farther out of the crusting dream.

===Chinese===
纳利克伊德尤（Nalik’ideyu，「走岭少女」）是那只雌兽，一路牧着他前行，从不离开他身边太远。纳金尔塔（Naginlta，「先行探路者」）是那只雄兽，干的正是名字里那桩事：隔上一阵便消失，随后归来，面对他和自己的伴侣，仿佛在传达旅途必需的某种通报。

此刻寻到特拉维斯跟前的正是纳利克伊德尤，她喘着气，红舌头耷拉在嘴外。不是累的——这一点他很确定。不，她是兴奋而热切……在打猎！就是这样——一场围猎！

一个念头挟着兽性的力量击中他，特拉维斯自己的舌头也舔过嘴唇。前方有肉——肥美、新鲜——活着的肉，正等着被宰杀。他体内那股贪婪的饥饿醒了，把他从结壳的迷梦里又摇醒了一层。

===Original===
His hands went to his waist, but the groping fingers did not find what vague memory told him should be there﻿—a belt, heavy with knife in sheath.

He examined his own body with attention to find he was adequately covered by breeches of a smooth, dull brown material which blended well with the vegetation about him. He wore a loose shirt, belted in at the narrow waist by a folded strip of cloth, the ends of which fluttered free. On his feet were tall moccasins, the leg pieces extending some distance up his calves, the toes turned up in rounded points.

Some of this he found familiar, but these were fragments of memory; again his mind fitted one picture above another. One thing he did know for sure﻿—he had no weapons. And that realization struck home with a thrust of real and terrible fear which tore away more of the bewilderment cloaking his mind.

===Chinese===
他的手摸向腰间，摸索的手指却没有找到模糊记忆中该在那里的东西——一条腰带，坠着入鞘的重刀。

他仔细检视自己的身体，发现身上的遮蔽倒算齐全：一条质地平滑、暗棕色的裤子，与周围的草木融得很好。他穿着宽松的衬衫，窄腰处用一条折起的布带束住，带子的两端随风飘着。脚上是高帮鹿皮软鞋（moccasin），靴筒上延到小腿好一段，鞋头翘起圆圆的尖。

这些物件有些他觉得眼熟，但都只是记忆的碎片；他的头脑又一次把一张画面叠上另一张。有一件事他确凿无疑——他没有武器。这个认识带着一记真切而可怕的恐惧扎进心底，又撕去了裹着他心智的那团迷惘的一层。

===Original===
Nalik’ideyu was impatient. Having advanced a step or two, she now looked back at him over her shoulder, yellow eyes slitted, her demand on him as instant and real as if she had voiced understandable words. Meat was waiting, and she was hungry. Also she expected Travis to aid in the hunt﻿—at once.

Though he could not match her fluid grace in moving through the grass, Travis followed her, keeping to cover. He shook his head vigorously, in spite of the stab of pain the motion cost him, and paid more attention to his surroundings. It was apparent that the earth under him, the grass around, the valley of the golden haze, were all real, not part of a dream. Therefore that other countryside which he kept seeing in a ghostly fashion was a hallucination.

Even the air which he drew into his lungs and expelled again, had a strange smell, or was it taste? He could not be sure which. He knew that hypno-training could produce queer side effects, but﻿ ﻿… this﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
纳利克伊德尤不耐烦了。她已先行一两步，此刻回头越过肩膀望他，黄眼睛眯成一线；她对他的催促即刻而真切，仿佛说出了能听懂的人话。肉在等着，她饿了。而且她指望特拉维斯立刻搭手围猎——马上。

虽学不来她穿行草丛时行云流水的身姿，特拉维斯仍跟了上去，借掩护而行。他狠狠摇头，全不顾这一动引来的刺痛，把更多注意力放在周遭环境上。显然，身下的大地、四周的草丛、金雾弥漫的山谷，都真实不虚，并非梦境的一部分。那么，那片他眼里不断幽幽浮现的另一种乡野，便是幻觉了。

就连吸进肺里又吐出来的空气，都带着一种奇异的气味——或者说，是味道？他分不清究竟是哪种。他知道催眠训练（hypno-training）会生出古怪的副作用，可是……这个……

===Original===
Travis paused, staring unseeingly before him at the grass still waving from the coyote’s passage. Hypno-training! What was that? Now three pictures fought to focus in his mind: the two landscapes which did not match and a shadowy third. He shook his head again, his hands to his temples. This﻿—this only was real: the ground, the grass, the valley, the hunger in him, the hunt waiting﻿ ﻿…

He forced himself to concentrate on the immediate present and the portion of world he could see, feel, scent, which lay here and now about him.

The grass grew shorter as he proceeded in Nalik’ideyu’s wake. But the haze was not thinning. It seemed to hang in patches, and when he ventured through the edge of a patch it was like creeping through a fog of golden, dancing motes with here and there a glittering speck whirling and darting like a living thing. Masked by the stuff, Travis reached a line of brush and sniffed.

===Chinese===
特拉维斯顿住脚步，视而不见地瞪着前方——郊狼过处，草浪犹自起伏。催眠训练！那是什么？此刻有三幅画面在他脑中争夺焦点：两幅对不上的风景，还有第三幅影影绰绰的。他又摇了摇头，双手抵住太阳穴。这个——只有这个才是真的：脚下的大地、四围的草、山谷、腹中的饥饿、等着开场的围猎……

他强迫自己集中心神，专注于眼前此刻、这方看得见摸得着嗅得出的世界——它就在此地、就在此刻，环围着他。

他尾随纳利克伊德尤前行，脚下的草渐渐矮了下去。可雾霭并未变薄。它像是一团一团悬在半空；当他冒险穿过一团雾的边缘时，就像匍匐爬过一片金色的、飞舞的光尘，间或有一粒闪亮的微屑打着旋、蹿着跳，活像个小生灵。借着这层东西的掩蔽，特拉维斯摸到一线灌木前，嗅了嗅。

===Original===
It was a warm scent, a heavy odor he could not identify and yet one he associated with a living creature. Flat to earth, he pushed head and shoulders under the low limbs of the bush to look ahead.

Here was a space where the fog did not hold, a pocket of earth clear under the morning sun. And grazing there were three animals. Again shock cleared a portion of Travis’ bemused brain.

They were about the size, he thought, of antelopes, and they had a general resemblance to those beasts in that they had four slender legs, a rounded body, and a head. But they had alien features, so alien as to hold him in open-mouthed amazement.

===Chinese===
那是一股温热的气味，一种沉重得叫他无从辨认的气味，却又让他联想到某个活物。他贴伏于地，把头和肩膀从灌木低垂的枝条下探进去，向前张望。

这里有一小片雾霭不到的空地，一方沐浴在朝阳下的净土。三只兽正在那里吃草。又一次，震动拂亮了特拉维斯恍惚头脑的一角。

它们的个头，他想，跟羚羊差不多；大体上也与羚羊相像：四条细腿、一副滚圆的身躯、一颗头。可它们的模样处处透着异星味，异得让他张口结舌、惊叹不已。

===Original===
The bodies had bare spots here and there, and patches of creamy﻿—fur? Or was it hair which hung in strips, as if the creatures had been partially plucked in a careless fashion? The necks were long and moved about in a serpentine motion, as though their spines were as limber as reptiles’. On the end of those long and twisting necks were heads which also appeared more suitable to another species﻿—broad, rather flat, with a singular toadlike look﻿—but furnished with horns set halfway down the nose, horns which began in a single root and then branched into two sharp points.

They were unearthly! Again Travis blinked, brought his hand up to his head as he continued to view the browsers. There were three of them: two larger and with horns, the other a smaller beast with less of the ragged fur and only the beginning button of a protuberance on the nose; it was probably a calf.

One of those mental alerts from the coyotes broke his absorption. Nalik’ideyu was not interested in the odd appearance of the grazing creatures; she was intent upon their usefulness in another way﻿—as a full and satisfying meal﻿—and she was again impatient with him for his dull response.

===Chinese===
躯体上东一块西一块地裸着，间杂一片片乳色的——毛？还是一绺绺垂挂的毛发，仿佛这些生物曾被漫不经心地拔去了一部分？颈子很长，游走扭动如同蛇行，好像它们的脊梁柔软得如同爬虫。在那长而扭摆的颈端，顶着的脑袋也像是更适合安在别的物种身上——又宽又扁，透着一股蛙似的怪相——鼻梁中段却生着角，一角自单根而出，又岔成两支尖锋。

它们绝非尘世之物！特拉维斯又眨了眨眼，抬手按住头，继续打量这几只食草兽。一共三只：两只大些，带着角；另一只小些，乱蓬蓬的毛少一些，鼻上只有个初萌的瘤状小突——多半是头幼崽。

郊狼发来的一道心念警讯打断了他的出神。纳利克伊德尤对这些食草兽的怪模样毫无兴趣；她惦记的是它们另一种用处——一顿丰盛餍足的美餐——而他反应迟钝，又惹得她不耐烦起来。

===Original===
His examination took a more practical turn. An antelope’s defense was speed, though it could be tricked into hunting range through its inordinate curiosity. The slender legs of these beasts suggested a like degree of speed, and Travis had no weapons at all.

Those nose horns had an ugly look; this thing might be a fighter rather than a runner. But the suggestion which had flashed from coyote to him had taken root. Travis was hungry, he was a hunter, and here was meat on the hoof, queer as it looked.

Again he received a message. Naginlta was on the opposite side of the clearing. If the creatures depended on speed, then Travis believed they could probably outrun not only him but the coyotes as well﻿—which left cunning and some sort of plan.

===Chinese===
他的观察转上了更实际的方向。羚羊保命靠的是速度，尽管它那没个够的好奇心也会把它骗进猎程之内。这几只兽的细胞提示着同等程度的脚力，而特拉维斯手无寸铁。

鼻上那对角瞧着凶险；这东西兴许是个斗士，而非善跑之辈。可从郊狼闪进他心里的那个念头已经扎了根。特拉维斯饿了，他是个猎手，眼前就是长着蹄子的肉——模样再怪也是肉。

他又收到一道讯息。纳金尔塔在林间空地的另一侧。倘若这些生灵仰仗速度，那么特拉维斯相信，它们多半跑得过他，也跑得过郊狼——如此一来，能靠的只剩狡计和某种筹划了。

===Original===
Travis glanced at the cover where he knew Nalik’ideyu crouched and from which had come that flash of agreement. He shivered. These were truly no animals, but *ga-n*, *ga-n* of power! And as *ga-n* he must treat them, accede to their will. Spurred by that, the Apache gave only flicks of attention to the browsers while at the same time he studied the part of the landscape uncovered by mist.

Without weapons or speed, they must conceive a trap. Again Travis sensed that agreement which was *ga-n* magic, and with it the strong impression urging him to the right. He was making progress with skill he did not even recognize and which he had never been conscious of learning.

The bushes and small, droop-limbed trees, their branches not clothed with leaves from proper twigs but with a reddish bristly growth protruding directly from their surfaces, made a partial wall for the pocket-sized meadow. That screen reached a rocky cleft where the mist curled in a long tongue through a wall twice Travis’ height. If the browsers could be maneuvered into taking the path through that cleft﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
特拉维斯瞥了一眼他知道纳利克伊德尤伏着的那处掩护，方才那道应允的意念便是从那里传来的。他打了个寒噤。这两位当真不是走兽，而是*ga-n*，有法力的*ga-n*！既为*ga-n*，他必得以礼相待、顺其意而行。受此鞭策，这阿帕奇人只分出一星半点的注意给那几只食草兽，同时细究着雾霭揭开的那部分地形。

没有武器，也没有脚力，他们就得设一个陷阱。特拉维斯又一次感知到那声应允——那是*ga-n*的法术——随之而来一股强烈的意念，催他向右。他正以自己都未曾辨认、也从不记得学过的本事推进着这一切。

灌木和枝条低垂的小树——枝上不长正经的嫩枝托叶，只直接从皮面戳出一簇簇红色硬毛——给这方寸小草场围了半圈墙。那道屏障延伸到一处岩石裂谷，雾霭正从一道两倍于特拉维斯身高的石壁间，卷着长舌穿隙而过。倘若能把食草兽诓进那条穿谷的道……

===Original===
Travis searched about him, and his hands closed upon the oldest weapon of his species, a stone pulled from an earth pocket and balanced neatly in the palm of his hand. It was a long chance but his best one.

The Apache took the first step on a new and fearsome road. These *ga-n* had put their thoughts﻿—or their desires﻿—into his mind. Could he so contact them in return?

With the stone clenched in his fist, his shoulders back against the wall not too far from the cleft opening, Travis strove to think out, clearly and simply, this poor plan of his. He did not know that he was reacting the way scientists deep space away had hoped he might. Nor did Travis guess that at this point he had already traveled far beyond the expectations of the men who had bred and trained the two mutant coyotes. He only believed that this might be the one way he could obey the wishes of the two spirits he thought far more powerful than any man. So he pictured in his mind the cleft, the running creatures, and the part the *ga-n* could play if they so willed.

===Chinese===
特拉维斯在四周搜寻，双手握上了他这个物种最古老的武器——一块从土窝里抠出的石头，在他掌心掂得四平八稳。胜算渺茫，却是他最好的一注。

这阿帕奇人踏上了一条崭新而可畏的路，迈出第一步。这些*ga-n*已把它们的念头——或者说欲望——放进了他的头脑。他能反过来这样联络它们吗？

石头攥在拳心，肩背抵着离谷口不远的石壁，特拉维斯竭力把自己那个蹩脚计划想得清楚又简单。他不知道，自己正做出深空之外那些科学家所盼望的反应。他也猜不到，就在此刻，他已远远超出了培育、训练那两只变异郊狼之人的预期。他只是相信，这或许是他能满足那两位神灵之愿的唯一法子——在他心目中，它们远比任何人都强大。于是他在脑中描画裂谷、奔逃的兽群，还有*ga-n*们若肯出力时能扮演的角色。

===Original===
Assent﻿—in its way as loud and clear as if shouted. The man fingered the stone, weighed it. There would probably be just one moment when he could use it to effect, and he must be ready.

From this point he could no longer see the small meadow where the grazers were. But Travis knew, as well as if he watched the scene, that the coyotes were creeping in, belly flat to earth, adding a feline stealth and patience to their own cunning.

There! Travis’ head jerked, the alert had come, the drive was beginning. He tensed, gripping his stone.

===Chinese===
应允传来——以它自己的方式，洪亮清晰如呐喊。那人摩挲着石头，掂了掂分量。能奏效的时机多半只有一瞬，他必须备好。

从这里他已望不见食草兽所在的小草场。但特拉维斯了如指掌，仿佛亲眼看着那一幕：郊狼们正肚皮贴地潜行而进，把猫科的潜匿与耐心叠加到它们天生的狡黠之上。

来了！特拉维斯的头猛地一扬——警讯已至，驱猎开场。他绷紧身子，攥紧石头。

===Original===
A yapping bark was answered by a sound he could not describe, a noise which was neither cough nor grunt but a combination of both. Again a yap-yap﻿…

A toad-head burst through the screen of brush, the double horn on its nose festooned with a length of grass torn up by the roots. Wide eyes﻿—milky and seeming to be without pupils﻿—fastened on Travis, but he could not be sure the thing saw him, for it kept on, picking up speed as it approached the cleft. Behind it ran the calf, and that guttural cry was bubbling from its broad flat lips.

The long neck of the adult writhed, the frog-head swung closer to the ground so that the twin points of the horn were at a slant﻿—aimed now at Travis. He had been right in his guess at their deadliness, but he had only a fleeting chance to recognize that fact as the thing bore down, its whole attitude expressing the firm intention of goring him.

===Chinese===
一声短吠得到一声他无法形容的回应——那动静既非咳嗽也非咕噜，却兼而有之。又是吠吠两声……

一颗蛙头撞破灌木屏障窜出，鼻上的双角还挂着一段连根扯起的草。一双凸眼——乳白浑浊，仿佛没有瞳仁——盯上了特拉维斯，可他拿不准那东西是否真看见了他，因为它径直向前，越临裂谷越快。幼崽跟在后面跑，宽扁的唇间咕噜噜地冒出那喉音。

成兽的长颈拧动着，蛙头压向地面，双角的两个尖锋斜成一线——这会儿正对着特拉维斯。他早先猜测这对角致命，此刻那东西挟着全副姿态直压过来、明明白白表示要拿角挑他，他只有一瞬工夫印证这份猜测。

===Original===
He hurled his stone and then flung his body to one side, stumbling and rolling into the brush where he fought madly to regain his feet, expecting at any moment to feel trampling hoofs and thrusting horns. There was a crash to his right, and the bushes and grass were wildly shaken.

On his hands and knees the Apache retreated, his head turned to watch behind him. He saw the flirt of a triangular flap-tail in the mouth of the cleft. The calf had escaped. And now the threshing in the bushes stilled.

Was the thing stalking him? He got to his feet, for the first time hearing clearly the continued yapping, as if a battle was in progress. Then the second of the adult beasts came into view, backing and turning, trying to keep lowered head with menacing double horn always pointed to the coyotes dancing a teasing, worrying circle about it.

===Chinese===
他掷出石头，随即整个人向一侧猛扑，跌跌撞撞滚进灌木丛，发疯似的想重新站起，时时刻刻等着挨上践踏的蹄、捅来的角。他右侧轰然一声巨响，灌木和草丛疯狂地摇晃起来。

那阿帕奇人手脚并用地后撤，头扭过来盯住身后。他看见三角扇尾在谷口一闪。幼崽逃掉了。而此刻，灌木里的扑腾声静了下去。

那东西在悄悄逼他？他站起身，头一回清清楚楚听见持续的吠声，仿佛一场鏖战正酣。随后，第二头成兽进入视野：它且退且转，竭力压低头颅，让威慑的双角始终对准绕着它跳逗扰袭、兜着圈子纠缠的两只郊狼。

===Original===
One of the coyotes flung up its head, looked upslope, and barked. Then, as one, both rushed the fighting beast, but for the first time from the same side, leaving it a clear path to retreat. It made a rush before which they fled easily, and then it whirled with a speed and grace, which did not fit its ungainly, ill-proportioned body, and jumped toward the cleft, the coyotes making no effort to hinder its escape.

Travis came out of cover, approaching the brush which had concealed the crash of the other animal. The actions of the coyotes had convinced him that there was no danger now; they would never have allowed the escape of their prey had the first beast not been in difficulties.

His shot with the stone, the Apache decided as he stood moments later surveying the twitching crumpled body, must have hit the thing in the head, stunning it. Then the momentum of its charge had carried it full force against the rock to kill it. Blind luck﻿—or the power of the *ga-n*? He pulled back as the coyotes came padding up shoulder to shoulder to inspect the kill. It was truly more theirs than his.

===Chinese===
一只郊狼扬起头，朝坡上望了一眼，吠了一声。随后两狼齐动，一齐扑向那头应战的兽——却是头一回从同一侧进攻，给它让出一条退路。它猛冲过来，两狼轻松闪开；它随即一个回旋，那份迅捷与优美全然不配它笨拙失称的身躯，纵身扑向裂谷；郊狼也不再去阻它的逃路。

特拉维斯出了掩体，走向那片遮住了另一头兽坠响的灌木。郊狼的举动让他确信眼下已无危险：若非第一头兽已经不济，它们绝不会放走自己的猎物。

片刻后，那阿帕奇人站着审视那具抽搐瘫软的尸体，心中断定：他那一石必是击中了兽头，把它打懵了；随后冲势的余劲把它整个带上岩石，撞了个正着，一命呜呼。纯粹的走运——还是*ga-n*的法力？两只郊狼并肩踏步上前查看猎获，他退到一旁。说实在的，这收获更多属于它们，而非他。

===Original===
Their prey yielded not only food but a weapon for Travis. Instead of the belt knife he had remembered having, he was now equipped with two. The double horn had been easy to free from the shattered skull, and some careful work with stones had broken off one prong at just the angle he wanted. So now he had a short and a longer tool, defense. At least they were better than the stone with which he had entered the hunt.

Nalik’ideyu pushed past him to lap daintily at the water. Then she sat up on her haunches, watching Travis as he smoothed the horn with a stone.

“A knife,” he said to her, “this will be a knife. And﻿—” he glanced up, measuring the value of the wood represented by trees and bushes﻿—“then a bow. With a bow we shall hunt better.”

===Chinese===
他们的猎获给特拉维斯提供的不止是吃食，还有武器。他记忆中那把腰刀没有了着落，如今他有了两把。双角从碎裂的头骨上取下来并不费事，再用石头细细敲凿，把一支角尖恰在他想要的斜度上断了下来。于是他有了长短两件家伙，算作防身。至少，总强过他入场围猎时那块石头。

纳利克伊德尤从他身边挤过去，斯斯文文地舔饮泉水。随后她后腿蹲坐起来，看特拉维斯用石头打磨角刃。

「一把刀，」他对她说，「这就会是一把刀。还有——」他抬眼估量着林木灌木所代表的木材价值——「然后再造一张弓。有了弓，咱们打猎就更顺手了。」

===Original===
The coyote yawned, her yellow eyes half closed, her whole pose one of satisfaction and contentment.

“A knife,” Travis repeated, “and a bow.” He needed weapons; he had to have them!

Why? His hand stopped scraping. Why? The toad-faced double horn had been quick to attack, but Travis could have avoided it, and it had not hunted him first. Why was he ridden by this fear that he must not be unarmed?

===Chinese===
郊狼打了个哈欠，黄眼睛半阖，通身的姿态写满餍足与安适。

「一把刀，」特拉维斯重复道，「还有一张弓。」他需要武器；他非有不可！

为什么？他打磨的手停了。为什么？那蛙脸双角兽出手虽快，特拉维斯本可以避开，何况先动手追猎的也不是它。这份「绝不可手无寸铁」的恐惧，为什么死死骑在他心头？

===Original===
He dipped his hand into the pool of the spring and lifted the water to cool his sweating face. The coyote moved, turned around in the grass, crushing down the growth into a nest in which she curled up, head on paws. But Travis sat back on his heels, his now idle hands hanging down between his knees, and forced himself to the task of sorting out jumbled memories.

This landscape was wrong﻿—totally unlike what it should be﻿—but it was real. He had helped kill this alien creature. He had eaten its meat, raw. Its horn lay within touch now. All that was real and unchangeable. Which meant that the rest of it, that other desert world in which he had wandered with his kind, ridden horses, raided invading men of another race, that was not real﻿—or else far, far removed from where he now sat.

Yet there had been no dividing line between those two worlds. One moment he had been in the desert place, returning from a successful foray against the Mexicans. Mexicans! Travis caught at that identification, tried to use it as a thread to draw closer to the beginning of his mystery.

===Chinese===
他把手浸进泉潭，捧水拍在汗涔涔的脸上降温。郊狼动了动，在草丛里转了个身，把草压成一个窝，蜷了进去，头枕在前爪上。特拉维斯却蹲坐在脚跟上，此刻闲下的双手垂在膝间，强迫自己去理清那一团乱麻般的记忆。

这片山川不对——全然不是它该有的模样——却是真实的。他参与了猎杀这只异星生物。他吃过它的肉，生的。它的角此刻就搁在伸手可及的地方。这一切都真实而不容更改。这就意味着，其余那些——他在另一个荒漠世界里与族人游荡、骑马、袭掠入侵异族的那一切——并不真实；或者说，离他此刻端坐之地隔着远而又远的距离。

可那两个世界之间并没有分界线。前一刻他还在那片荒漠，正从一场对墨西哥人的成功突袭中返程。墨西哥人！特拉维斯一把抓住这个名目，想把它当一根线头，牵出自己这团谜的开端。

===Original===
Mexicans﻿ ﻿… And he was an Apache, one of the Eagle people, one who rode with Cochise. No!

Sweat again beaded his face where the water had cooled it. He was not of that past. He was Travis Fox, of the very late twentieth century, not a nomad of the middle nineteenth! He was of Team A of the project!

The Arizona desert and then this! From one to the other in an instant. He looked about him in rising fear. Wait! He had been in the dark when he got out of the desert, lying in a box. Getting out, he had crawled down a passage to reach moonlight, strange moonlight.

===Chinese===
墨西哥人……而他是个阿帕奇，鹰族（Eagle people）的一员，随科奇西（Cochise）并辔征战的人。不！

刚被泉水拍凉的脸上又沁出了汗珠。他不属于那个过去。他是特拉维斯·福克斯，二十世纪末叶的人，不是十九世纪中叶的游牧民！他是项目A队的队员！

亚利桑那的荒漠，然后是这里！一个瞬息便从这头到了那头。他环顾四周，恐惧渐涨。等等！离开那片荒漠时他身处黑暗，躺在一具箱子里。爬出来后，他沿一条通道爬行，才见到月光——陌生的月光。

===Original===
A box in which he had lain, a passage with smooth metallic walls, and an alien world at the end of it.

The coyote’s ears twitched, her head came up, she was staring at the man’s drawn face, at his eyes with their core of fear. She whined.

Travis caught up the two pieces of horn, thrust them into his sash belt, and got to his feet. Nalik’ideyu sat up, her head cocked a little to one side. As the man turned to seek his own back trail she padded along in his wake and whined for Naginlta. But Travis was more intent now on what he must prove to himself than he was on the actions of the two animals.

===Chinese===
一具他躺过的箱子，一条金属壁光滑的通道，而通道尽头，是一个异星世界。

郊狼的耳朵一颤，头抬了起来；她盯着男人紧绷的脸，盯着他眼底那点恐惧的核。她呜咽了一声。

特拉维斯抓起两截兽角，插进腰间的束带，站起身。纳利克伊德尤坐了起来，头微微偏向一侧。当那人转身去寻自己来时的路径，她轻步跟在后头，呜咽着唤纳金尔塔。可特拉维斯此刻更专注于他必须向自己证明的事，反倒顾不上两头兽的举动。

===Original===
It was a wandering trail, and now he did not question his skill in being able to follow it so unerringly. The sun was hot. Winged things buzzed from the bushes, small scuttling things fled from him through the tall grass. Once Naginlta growled a warning which led them all to a detour, and Travis might not have picked up the proper trace again had not the coyote scout led him to it.

“Who are you?” he asked once, and then guessed it would have better been said, “What are you?” These were not animals, or rather they were more than the animals he had always known. And one part of him, the part which remembered the desert rancherias where Cochise had ruled, said they were spirits. Yet that other part of him﻿ ﻿… Travis shook his head, accepting them now for what they were﻿—welcome company in an alien place.

The day wore on close to sunset, and still Travis followed that wandering trail. The need which drove him kept him going through the rough country of hills and ravines. Now the mist lifted above towering walls of mountains very near him, yet not the mountains of his memory. These were dull brown, with a forbidding look, like sun-dried skulls baring teeth in warning against all comers.

===Chinese===
那是一条七弯八绕的踪迹，而如今他已不再怀疑自己何以能这样分毫不差地循迹而行。日头毒辣。有翅的东西嗡嗡飞出灌木，细碎疾窜的小生灵穿过高草从他面前逃开。有一回纳金尔塔低吼示警，引得众人绕道而行；若非那头郊狼斥候领着，特拉维斯兴许再也找不回正路。

「你们是谁？」他问过一回，随即又想，或许更该问：「你们是什么？」它们不是兽，或者说，远不止是他素来认得的那些兽。而他身上有一部分——记得科奇西治下荒漠聚落（rancheria）的那部分——说它们是神灵。可另一部分的他……特拉维斯摇了摇头，此刻只按它们的本来面目接纳它们——异乡里可亲可感的旅伴。

天色磨到将近日落，特拉维斯仍循着那条曲曲折折的踪迹走。那股驱策他的需要，撑着他翻过丘壑纵横的险地。此刻雾霭已升到近在咫尺的巍峨山墙之上——却不是他记忆中的山。这些山暗棕色，一副拒人的凶相，像晒干的骷髅龇着牙，警告一切来者。

===Original===
With great difficulty, Travis topped a rise. Ahead against the skyline stood both coyotes. And, as the man joined them, first one and then the other flung back its head and sounded the sobbing, shattering cry which had been a part of that other life.

The Apache looked down. His puzzle was answered in part. The wreckage crumpled on the mountain side was identifiable﻿—a spaceship! Cold fear gripped him and his own head went back; from between his tight lips came a cry as desolate and despairing as the one the animals had voiced.

===Chinese===
特拉维斯艰难地攀上一道高坡。前方，天际线上并立着两只郊狼。当那人走到它们身边，它们先后仰起头，发出那种呜咽欲绝、摧人心神的嗥叫——那是另一种生命里曾有的一部分。

阿帕奇人朝下望去。他的谜团解开了一角。蜷伏在山坡上的残骸可以辨认——一艘星船（spaceship）！冰冷的恐惧攫住了他，他的头也仰了起来；紧闭的双唇间迸出一声嗥叫，与两头兽方才发出的那声一样荒凉、一样绝望。

## IV / IV

===Original===
Fire, mankind’s oldest ally, weapon, tool, leaped high before the naked stone of the mountain side. Men sat cross-legged about it, fifteen of them. And behind, guarded by the flames and that somber circle, were the women. There was a uniformity in this gathering. The members were plainly all of the same racial stock, of medium height, stocky yet fined down to the peak of stamina and endurance, their skin brown, their shoulder-length hair black. And they were all young﻿—none over thirty, some still in their late teens. Alike, too, was a certain drawn look in their faces, a tenseness of the eyes and mouth as they listened to Travis.

“So we must be on Topaz. Do any of you remember boarding the ship?”

“No. Only that we awoke within it.” Across the fire one chin lifted; the eyes which caught Travis’ held a deep, smoldering anger. “This is more trickery of the Pinda-lick-o-yi, the White Eyes. Between us there has never been fair dealing. They have broken their promise as a man breaks a rotten stick, for their words are as rotten. And it was you, Fox, who brought us to listen to them.”

===Chinese===
火，人类最古老的盟友、武器与工具，在山壁裸岩前蹿得老高。男人们盘腿围火而坐，一共十五个。在他们身后、由火焰和那圈沉郁人墙守卫着的，是妇女。这群人有一种整齐划一的味道。成员显然全属同一种族：中等个头，敦实却精炼到了耐力与持久力的巅峰，棕色皮肤，齐肩黑发。而且个个年轻——没有过三十岁的，有的还不满二十岁。相像的还有他们脸上某种紧绷的神情，听特拉维斯说话时，眼睛和嘴角都带着张力。

「这么说，我们必定是在托帕兹了。你们有谁记得登船的经过吗？」

「没有。只记得是在船里醒来的。」火堆对面一张下巴扬起；迎上特拉维斯的那双眼睛，燃着深沉郁结的怒火。「这又是*Pinda-lick-o-yi*（白人）的另一桩诡计。我们之间从来没有过公道交易。他们撕毁诺言，就像人折断一根朽棍，因为他们的话本就朽烂。而当初领我们来听他们摆布的，正是你，狐狸（Fox）。」

===Original===
A stir about the circle, a murmur from the women.

“And do I not also sit here with you in this strange wilderness?” he countered.

“I do not understand,” another of the men held out his hand, palm up, in a gesture of asking﻿—“what has happened to us. We were in the old Apache world﻿ ﻿… I, Jil-Lee, was riding with Cuchillo Negro as we went down to the taking of Ramos. And then I was here, in a broken ship and beside me a dead man who was once my brother. How did I come out of the past of our people into another world across the stars?”

“Pinda-lick-o-yi tricks!” The first speaker spat into the fire.

===Chinese===
火圈一阵骚动，妇女中起了低语。

「难道我自己不也同你们一道坐在这片陌生的荒野里？」他反问道。

「我不明白，」另一名男子摊开手掌，做了个求告的姿势——「我们身上到底出了什么事。我们本在阿帕奇的旧世界里……我，吉尔-李（Jil-Lee），当时正随库奇洛·内格罗（Cuchillo Negro）驰骑，下山去攻取拉莫斯（Ramos）。然后我就到了这里，躺在一艘破船里，身边是一具尸体——他曾是我兄弟。我怎么会从族人的过去，跨进星空另一头的另一个世界？」

「*Pinda-lick-o-yi*的把戏！」最先说话那人朝火里啐了一口。

===Original===
“It was the Redax, I think,” Travis replied. “I heard Dr. Ashe discuss this. A new machine which could make a man remember not his own past, but the past of his ancestors. While we were on that ship we must have been under its influence, so we lived as our people lived a hundred years or more ago﻿—”

“And the purpose of such a thing?” Jil-Lee asked.

“To make us more like our ancestors perhaps. It is part of what they told us at the project. To venture into these new worlds requires a different type of man than lives on Terra today. Traits we have forgotten are needed to face the dangers of wild places.”

===Chinese===
「我想，那是雷达克斯。」特拉维斯答道。「我听阿什医生谈起过。一种新机器，能让人记起的不是自己的过去，而是祖先的过去。我们在船上时，必定一直在它的影响之下，所以我们过的，是一百年前乃至更久以前族人的生活——」

「这么一样东西，为的是什么？」吉尔-李问。

「也许是为了让我们更像我们的祖先。这也是项目里他们对我们说的一部分。要闯荡这些新世界，需要一种与今日地球人所不同的新人。要直面蛮荒之地的种种凶险，就得唤回我们已经遗忘的品性。」

===Original===
“You, Fox, have been beyond the stars before, and you found there were such dangers to face?”

“It is true. You have heard of the three worlds I saw when the ship from the old days took us off, unwilling, to the stars. Did you not all volunteer to pioneer in this manner so you could also see strange and new things?”

“But we did not agree to be returned to the past in medicine dreams and be sent unknowingly into space!”

Travis nodded. “Deklay is right. But I know no more than you why we were so sent, or why the ship crashed. We have found Dr. Ruthven’s body in the cabin with that new installation. Only we have discovered nothing else which tells us why we were brought here. With the ship broken, we must stay.”

===Chinese===
「你，狐狸，以前到过群星之外，你在那边发现过这类凶险吗？」

「确有其事。你们都听说过那三个世界——远古的飞船不由分说把我们带上星海时，我亲眼所见。你们当年不都是自愿报名，要以这种方式拓荒，好亲眼看看新奇的事物吗？」

「可我们没答应让人用迷药之梦送回过去，再糊里糊涂给发射到太空里来！」

特拉维斯点了点头。「德克雷（Deklay）说得对。可我们为什么被送来、船又为什么坠毁，我知道的并不比你们多。我们在装有那台新装置的舱室里找到了鲁思文医生的尸体。只是除此以外，我们再没发现任何东西能说明他们为什么把我们弄到这里。船已破了，我们只能留下。」

===Original===
They were silent now, men and women alike. Behind them lay several days of activity, nights of exhausted slumber. Against the cliff wall lay the packs of supplies they had salvaged from the wreck. By mutual consent they had left the vicinity of the broken globe, following their old custom of speedily withdrawing from a place of death.
